   121	Classic IIFE. `var Planner = window.Planner; var config = Planner.config; var C = Planner.components; var api = Planner.api;` then `Planner.registerScreen("board", render);`.
   122	
   123	### 2.1 `render(root, params)`
   124	
   125	- Fetch `api.fetchJson("/api/board")` → `{columns:[{state, cards:[{id,title,priority,deadline,project,has_pending_proposal,has_running_claim}]}]}`. (`board_view`, tickets/views.py 224–259: exactly six columns in `STATE_ORDER`, `dropped` never present, cards pre-sorted server-side.)
   126	- On rejection: `root.appendChild(C.errorLine(err))`; return.
   127	- On success: build the screen:
   128	```
   129	section.board-screen         [data-screen="board"]
   130	  div.board                                              // horizontal scroll container
   131	    // per column, in served order:
   132	    div.board-column          [data-column=<col.state>]
   133	      div.board-column-head   → stateLabel(col.state) + " " + col.cards.length   // name + count
   134	      div.board-column-cards
   135	        // per card → cardAnchor(card)   (below)
   136	        // if col.cards empty → div.quiet-line "(empty)"
   137	```
   138	- Append `section` to `root`.
   139	
   140	### 2.2 `cardAnchor(card)` → DOM node
   141	
   142	Reuse `C.entityRow` then decorate (entityRow does not set `data-*`):
   143	```
   144	var chips = [
   145	  C.chip("priority", card.priority),
   146	  card.deadline ? C.chip("deadline", card.deadline) : null,   // plain; NO overdue flag (server sends none; no client date math)
   147	  card.project  ? C.chip("project",  card.project)  : null,
   148	  card.has_pending_proposal ? marker("pending-proposal") : null,
   149	  card.has_running_claim    ? marker("running-claim")    : null
   150	];
   151	var a = C.entityRow({ href: config.ROUTES.ticketPrefix + card.id, title: card.title, chips: chips });
   152	a.setAttribute("data-card", "");
   153	a.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", card.id);
   154	return a;
   155	```
   156	`marker(v)` = `var c = C.chip(v); c.setAttribute("data-marker", v); return c;` — reuses the existing chip marker variants (`chip--pending-proposal` "proposal pending", `chip--running-claim` "● running", components.js 142–148/170–172). No drag-and-drop; the whole card is the click-through anchor (`entityRow` is a native `<a>`).
   157	
   158	---
   159	
   160	## 3. `assets/screens-ticket.js`  (§10.4 Ticket)
   161	
   162	Classic IIFE, same header aliases. `Planner.registerScreen("ticket", render);`. Private consts: `LINK_KINDS = ["belongs_to","parent_child","blocks","relates"];`. Private helpers listed in §3.3.
   163	
   164	### 3.1 `render(root, params)`
   165	
   166	`var id = params.id;`
   167	
   168	Parallel fetch (single Promise.all so one failure is one error, and the four are independent):
   169	```
   170	Promise.all([
   171	  api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id),          // detail  (ticket_detail)
   172	  api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/events"),
   173	  api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/runs"),
   174	  api.fetchJson("/api/sprints")                 // {sprints:[{id,name,…}]}
   175	]).then(onLoaded, function(err){ root.appendChild(C.errorLine(err)); });
   176	```
   177	`not_found` on the detail rejects the whole Promise.all → single `errorLine`, no crash (constraint 5). `onLoaded([detail, evs, runs, sprints])` builds the screen root and appends the panels in the order below, then appends to `root`.
   178	
   179	`detail` shape (ticket_detail, tickets/views.py 121–160): the full `ticket_json` (id,title,state,priority,deadline,project,sprint_item_id,sprint_id,recap,ceiling,at_cap,auto_blocked,consecutive_failures,chat_session_key,alias,fields,claim_expires,claim_active,created_at,updated_at) **plus** `blocked`, `effective_sprint_id`, `links[]`, `day_ids[]`, `run_summary`. `fields` is `{success,approach,plan,result}` each `{value,proposal,notes}`.
   180	
   181	Screen root:
   182	```
   183	section.ticket-screen   [data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id=<id>][data-state=<detail.state>]
   184	```
   185	
   186	### 3.2 Sections, in order
   187	
   188	**(1) Header** — `C.panel(detail.title, [chipsRow])` (or a bespoke `ticket-header` block titled by the ticket title). Chips row:
   189	- `chip("state", detail.state)`, `chip("priority", detail.priority)`, `detail.project && chip("project", detail.project)`, `detail.deadline && chip("deadline", detail.deadline)`.
   190	- Markers (each `chip(...)` with `data-marker` set):
   191	  - `detail.claim_active` → `marker("running-claim")`.
   192	  - `detail.auto_blocked` → `marker("auto-blocked")` (chip variant `auto-blocked`, "auto-blocked").
   193	  - `detail.blocked` (link-blocked) → `var b = chip("blocked", "blocked"); b.setAttribute("data-marker","blocked");` — `"blocked"` is not a known chip variant, so it hits the default branch (components.js 173–174): a plain `.chip` with text "blocked". (No existing marker variant means "link-blocked"; the default chip is the closest, per constraint 5.)
   194	- Header markers reuse `[data-marker=…]` (constraint 6). No pending-proposal chip in the header — the pending proposal lives in its field section.
   195	
   196	**(2)–(5) Field sections** success / approach / plan / result — one `C.panel(fieldName, body)` each, panel body element carries `[data-field=<name>]`:
   197	- `C.markdownBlock(detail.fields[name].value)` — value as markdown (§10.4 "value rendered as markdown"; §4.2).
   198	- If `detail.fields[name].proposal` is non-null → append `C.proposalCard({…})` (T15, §7). ALL non-null proposals render, not just the gating one; Accept on a non-gating field calls the same endpoint and the server decides (constraint 5; resolution.py `decide_accept` sets value only for non-gating, requires the grant pair only on the gating branch, lines 162–174). onAccept → `POST /api/tickets/{id}/accept/{name}` body `{edited_body, next_ceiling, at_cap}` (AcceptBody, tickets/api.py 117–121, 304–322; `next_ceiling` a TicketState value or `"none"`, `at_cap` `"stop"|"propose"`).
   199	- Notes → `C.fieldEditor(detail.fields[name].notes, function(note){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/notes/"+name, {method:"PUT", body:{note:note}}); })` (§4.2 notes; PUT /notes/{field}, NoteBody `{note}`, tickets/api.py 333–343).
   200	
   201	**(6) Recap** — `C.panel("Recap", [...])`, body `[data-recap]`: `C.markdownBlock(detail.recap)` + `C.fieldEditor(detail.recap, function(body){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/recap", {method:"PUT", body:{body:body}}); })` (§10.4 recap; PUT /recap, RecapBody `{body}`).
   202	
   203	**(7) State control** — `C.panel("State", [ C.stateControl({ state: detail.state, autoBlocked: detail.auto_blocked, onJump, onDrop, onUnblock }) ])`:
   204	- `onJump = function(to){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/state", {method:"POST", body:{to:to}}); }` (POST /state, StateBody `{to}`, human-only).
   205	- `onDrop = function(){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/drop", {method:"POST"}); }` (POST /drop).
   206	- `onUnblock = function(){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/unblock", {method:"POST"}); }` (POST /unblock; clears `auto_blocked`, tickets/api.py 403–409). Unblock button only renders when `detail.auto_blocked` (component handles it).
   207	
   208	**(8) Grant control** — `C.panel("Grant", [ C.grantControl({ ceiling: detail.ceiling, atCap: detail.at_cap, onSave: function(ceiling, atCap){ return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/"+id+"/grant", {method:"POST", body:{ceiling:ceiling, at_cap:atCap}}); } }) ])` (§4.3; POST /grant, GrantBody `{ceiling, at_cap}` — both required, server errors `grant_missing` if either absent, tickets/api.py 356–382).
   209	
   210	**(9) Copy ticket** — `C.panel("Copy", [copyButton])`. `copyButton` = `<button class="button" data-copy>` "Copy ticket". On click, in-flight:
   211	```
   212	fetchText("/api/tickets/"+id+"/copy-text")          // §7.6? no — plain text; MUST NOT use fetchJson (JSON.parse would reject text)
   213	  .then(copyToClipboard)                             // clipboard write
   214	  .then(function(){ flash button text → "Copied", restore after a short timeout },
   215	        function(err){ errorHost.prepend(C.errorLine(err)); })
   216	```
   217	`copy-text` returns `text/plain` (`PlainTextResponse`, tickets/api.py 424–426 → `copy_text`, views.py 184–218: title / state / priority / success / approach / plan / result / recap / links block). This is a read, not a WS-mutating action; no flush follows, so the "Copied" flash is the only feedback and is set directly (the sole sanctioned direct DOM tweak in the ticket screen; documented as such).
   218	
   219	**(10) Links** — `C.panel("Links", [linksList, addForm])`. `linksList` `[data-links]`:
   220	- Per `detail.links` entry `{from_id,to_id,kind}` → a row `[data-link-row][data-link-kind=<kind>]`:
   221	  - text: `kind` · `from_id` " → " far-end. Far end = the id that is not `id`; if it starts with `"t_"` render it as `<a href="#/ticket/"+farId>` (both `from_id` and `to_id` shown; only ticket ids get anchors — sprint-item/other ids render as plain text).
   222	  - Remove button → `DELETE /api/links?from_id=<>&to_id=<>&kind=<>` (query params, remove_link, tickets/api.py 438–444). Build the query with `encodeURIComponent`.
   223	- `addForm` `[data-link-add]`: `from_id` text input (prefilled with `id`), `to_id` text input, `kind` `<select>` over `LINK_KINDS`, Add button → `POST /api/links` body `{from_id, to_id, kind}` (add_link, tickets/api.py 429–435).
   224	
   225	**(11) Day / sprint assignment** —
   226	- Day `[data-day-assign]`: date `<input type="date">` defaulted to the client's today (`new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)`) + "Add to day" button → `POST /api/day/{isoDate}/tickets` body `{ticket_id: id}` (days/api.py 129–137; `{date}` accepts ISO or `today`; client sends ISO). Existing `detail.day_ids` each a row `[data-day-row]` showing the day id + Remove → `DELETE /api/day/{date}/tickets/{id}` where **`date = dayId.slice("day_".length)`** i.e. `dayId.slice(4)` (ids.py 30–31: `day_id` = `"day_" + isoDate`; e.g. `day_2026-07-05` → `2026-07-05`).
   227	- Sprint `[data-sprint-assign]`: if `detail.sprint_item_id != null` → plain text `"sprint: " + (detail.effective_sprint_id || "(none)")`, **no select** (server rejects `set_sprint` on parented tickets — `_set_project`/`set_sprint` guard, and sprint is derived through the item). Else → `<select>` over `sprints.sprints` (option value = `s.id`, label = `s.name`) plus a leading `"(none)"` option (value `""`), preselected to `detail.sprint_id`; Save → `PATCH /api/tickets/{id}` body `{sprint_id: value || null}` (patch_ticket, tickets/api.py 263–288).
   228	
   229	**(12) Run history** — `C.panel("Runs", [ C.runHistory(runs.runs) ])` (D11 #15).
   230	
   231	**(13) Event log** — `C.panel("Events", [ C.eventLog(evs.events) ])` (D11 #14).
   232	
   233	**(14) Chat** — `C.panel("Chat", [chatWrap])`. `chatWrap` = `<div data-chat>` containing `C.chatPanel({ entityId: id })` (T15, §7). The `[data-chat]` wrapper is T16's; T15's component owns its internal DOM/attributes.
   234	
   235	### 3.3 Private helpers in `screens-ticket.js`
   236	
   237	- `marker(v)` — same as board (`chip` + `data-marker`).
   238	- `fetchText(path)` — `return fetch(path).then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) return r.text().then(function(t){ throw errorFrom(r, t); }); return r.text(); });` — a tiny text fetch so `copy-text` is not run through `fetchJson`. (Reject shape need only carry `.code`/`.message` for `errorLine`; a minimal `{code:"http_error", message:"HTTP "+status}` is fine — do **not** modify api.js.)
   239	- `copyToClipboard(text)` — `if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);` else fallback: create a hidden `<textarea>`, set value, append, `select()`, `document.execCommand("copy")`, remove; resolve/reject a Promise accordingly.
   240	- `submit(button, errorHost, fn)` — the in-flight pattern for the link/day/sprint/copy buttons (same shape as `inFlight` in components.js; screens carry their own copy since the components helper is private to that file).
   241	- `selectEl(options, selectedValue)` — builds a `<select>` from `[{value,label}]`, preselecting `selectedValue`.
   242	
   243	---

   245	## 4. `assets/app.css` — additive sections (tokens-only)
   246	
   247	Append new blocks; every color/size/duration/font resolves through a `var(--token)` from `tokens.css` (permitted non-token literals: `0`, unitless ratios, percentages/viewport units, keywords, font-weights — matching app.css's own header rule, lines 1–4). New class names follow the kebab convention: `board-*`, `ticket-*`, `state-control-*`, `grant-control-*`, `run-history-*`, `event-log-*`.
   248	
   249	- **Board** — `.board { display: flex; gap: var(--space-4); overflow-x: auto; }` (horizontal scroll, constraint 8); `.board-column { min-width: calc(var(--space-7) * 5); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-2); }` (min-width = an existing space-token multiple; ≈240px); `.board-column-head { … }` (uses `--type-sm`, `--text-muted`, spacing tokens); `.board-column-cards { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); }`.
   250	- **Ticket** — single-column stack of `.panel`s (the panels already `margin-bottom: var(--space-4)`); no two-column layout (simplicity, constraint 8). `.ticket-header-chips { display: flex; gap: var(--space-1); flex-wrap: wrap; }`.
   251	- **State control** — `.state-control { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); flex-wrap: wrap; }` + `.state-control-select`/error styling from tokens.
   252	- **Grant control** — `.grant-control { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-3); flex-wrap: wrap; }`; `.grant-control-atcap { display: flex; gap: var(--space-2); }`.
   253	- **Run history / Event log** — row layouts: `.run-history-row`, `.event-log-row { display: flex; gap: var(--space-3); … }`; `.run-history-status`/`.event-log-kind` mono via `--font-mono`, `--type-xs`; times `--text-faint`.
   254	- **Links / day / sprint** — `.ticket-link-row`, `.ticket-day-row`, `.link-add`, `.day-assign`, `.sprint-assign` as flex rows with `--space-*` gaps.
   255	- Native `<select>`/`<input type="date"|"text">` inherit the existing `button, textarea { font: inherit; }` where relevant; add a shared `.select`/input rule if needed, tokens-only, mirroring `.field-editor-input` (app.css 200–216).
   256	
   257	No new token category is introduced (PRINCIPLES.md "New token categories require evidence of need"). If a genuinely new themable value is unavoidable, that is a blocker to raise, not an inline literal.

   189	## 10. The UI
   190	
   191	Six screens, served at `/`. Simplicity is the ruling aesthetic: fewer things done cleanly. Every screen renders from server JSON; refresh at any moment restores identical state.
   192	
   193	Design system (per PRINCIPLES.md, binding):
   194	- One token file, `assets/tokens.css`, owns everything themable: surfaces, text tokens, accent (bright/surface/text), radius scale, motion durations (fast/base/slow), spacing scale, border widths. The app's entire personality must be tunable by editing this one file. No new token categories without evidence of need.
   195	- Type: strict scale, five sizes maximum. Motion: entrances ease out, nothing bounces, all durations from motion tokens. Add nothing to a screen unless it makes the user feel something or a smart person genuinely needs it to understand the screen; no explanatory text for the obvious.
   196	- Before writing any component, record the component inventory for all five screens in decisions.md; build few, reuse hard, no near-duplicates.
   197	
   198	1. **Day (home)**: the brief (rendered markdown); the day-plan tree with per-node Accept / Invalidate and top-level Accept-all / Reject-all; the day's ticket list (ordered); a Review entry showing the pending count; and the day chat panel (Section 11) — quick capture happens here, by telling the planner, which files tickets/ideas via the CLI (Section 17).
   199	2. **Review**: the one-at-a-time review surface — one home of approvals (the Ticket screen is the other), and where the morning pass happens. Pending items — gating-field proposals, sprint-item status proposals, and `needs_review` results — presented one at a time, oldest first. Each shows the proposal rendered, a quick-edit textarea, Accept (with the mandatory grant-pair picker of Section 4.4: how far, and stop-vs-propose — plain pickers, including "no further"), Skip (item stays queued, move on), and a link to the full Ticket. Resolved items leave the queue immediately.
   200	3. **Board**: one column per ticket state (`dropped` hidden), cards show title, priority, deadline, project, pending-proposal marker, running-claim marker. No drag-and-drop in v1: card click opens Ticket.
   201	4. **Ticket**: all four fields as sections (value rendered as markdown; pending proposal shown with Accept/Edit, accepts carrying the onward grant; notes editable inline), recap, state control, ceiling and at-cap as plain pickers (no dial widgetry), a **Copy ticket** button (copies a plain-text block — title, state, priority, field values, recap, links — to the clipboard for pasting into any agent or editor), links, day/sprint assignment, run history, event log, and the chat panel (Section 11).
   202	5. **Sprint**: kickoff fields, items grouped by status (with ticket rollup counts and blockers-cleared flags), loose tickets, review fields (frozen states shown as locked).
   203	6. **Backlog & Ideas**: itemless sprint items list (by priority) and ideas list, each with create forms.
   204	
   205	## 11. Chat
   206	

 succeeded in 0ms:

   261	## 5. `orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/smoke.py`
   262	
   263	Model directly on `orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/smoke.py`: in-process uvicorn on a daemon thread, temp DB, `free_or(PORT)`, `wait_ready`, `ok NN —` numbered prints, `try/finally` teardown (close client/page/browser, stop playwright, `server.should_exit`, join, rmtree), final `SMOKE PASS (N checks)`, any failed assertion → traceback + exit 1.
   264	
   265	**Differences from T14's env (constraint 7):**
   266	- `PLAN_TEST_MODE = "1"` but **NO `PLAN_FAKE_NOW`** — `seed_demo` stamps real wall-clock (`time.time()`, `datetime.now()`, seed/demo.py 29–30); a fake clock would skew the day/deadline alignment the demo builds against `today`.
   267	- Keep `PLAN_DB_PATH`, `PLAN_PORT`, `PLAN_LOGS_DIR`, `PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH`, `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS="50"` as in T14 (tight flush cadence; ui_debounce stays 250).
   268	- Gateway resolves to the **echo fake** by default in test mode (`gateway_adapter` default `"auto"` → `fake` → `EchoGatewayAdapter`, online, `available=True`, replies `"echo: <text>"`; registry.py 61–69, fakes.py 86–99). So the chat assertion follows the **online** branch.
   269	
   270	**Setup (before the browser drives anything):**
   271	1. Boot server, `wait_ready`. Seed: `client.post("/api/seed", json={"demo": True})` → 200 (seed/api.py 37–58; requires empty DB, which the temp DB is).
   272	2. `client.get("/api/tickets")` → map title → id. Capture:
   273	   - `t_success` = "Draft the onboarding email success criteria." (needs_success, ceiling needs_success, at_cap propose)
   274	   - `t_impl`    = "Implement the demo feature slice." (in_progress, P0)
   275	   - `t_flaky`   = "Fix the flaky login test." (in_progress, P0)
   276	   - `t_review`  = "Review the analytics dashboard numbers." (needs_review — for the relates target)
   277	   - `t_dropped` = "Prototype the voice input toggle." (dropped — must be absent from the board)
   278	3. **(a) Park a pending proposal** on `t_success`: `client.post("/api/tickets/"+t_success+"/propose/success", json={"body":"Smoke success body."}, headers={"X-Plan-Actor":"agent"})` → 200. Plain-agent context (actor "agent", no claim → `is_claimed_agent` False, no `require_claim`; authctx 57–65). At ceiling `needs_success` with `at_cap=propose`, `success` is the gating field, advance-target `needs_approach` > ceiling → proposal parks (§4.4.2–3). Board `has_pending_proposal` = `gating_field_pending` → true.
   279	4. **(b) Live claim + two runs** on `t_impl`, via direct sqlite (sanctioned — smoke owns the temp DB, dispatcher out of scope): open `sqlite3.connect(db_path)`; `UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock='claim_smoke01', claim_expires=<now+3600> WHERE id=?`; `INSERT INTO runs (id,ticket_id,status,started_at,ended_at,summary,error,pid) VALUES ('run_smoke_run',?,'running',<now-120>,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL)` and `('run_smoke_done',?,'done',<now-600>,<now-300>,'demo done run',NULL,NULL)` (columns per db.py 128–138; status ∈ the CHECK set). commit, close. Board `has_running_claim` for `t_impl` = `has_active_claim` → true.
   280	5. **(c) Auto-block** `t_flaky`: `UPDATE tickets SET auto_blocked=1 WHERE id=?`, commit. (For the unblock round-trip; `t_flaky` is a `blocks` **source**, so it is not itself link-blocked.)
   281	6. Launch chromium; **grant clipboard perms** on the context: `context.grant_permissions(["clipboard-read","clipboard-write"])` (Playwright: create context, then page). `page.goto(base + "/")`, wait for `window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1`.
   282	
   283	**Assertions (Playwright DOM against `#/board` and `#/ticket/<id>`; number every one):**
   284	
   285	1. **Board columns + counts + dropped hidden.** goto `#/board`; wait for `[data-screen="board"]`. Assert exactly six `[data-column]`, in order `needs_success, needs_approach, needs_plan, in_progress, needs_review, done`. Card counts: needs_success 1, needs_approach 1, needs_plan 1, in_progress 2, needs_review 1, done 1. Assert `t_dropped`'s title text appears in **no** `[data-card]`.
   286	2. **Markers + card chips.** The `t_success` card (`[data-card][data-ticket-id=t_success]`) carries `[data-marker="pending-proposal"]`; the `t_impl` card carries `[data-marker="running-claim"]`. A card shows priority/deadline/project chips (assert the `t_success` card has a priority chip, a deadline chip, a project chip present).
   287	3. **Navigate via card click.** Click the `t_success` card; wait for `location.hash === "#/ticket/"+t_success`. Assert `[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id=t_success]`, `[data-state="needs_success"]`, and the `[data-field="success"]` section hosts the proposal card (T15's proposal element — see §7 assumed attribute `[data-proposal-accept]` inside `[data-field="success"]`).
   288	4. **Accept-with-grant from the ticket screen.** Inside `[data-field="success"]`, use T15's grant-pair picker (assumed `[data-grant-pick-ceiling]` select + `[data-grant-pick-at-cap]` radios, §7): choose a `next_ceiling` (e.g. `needs_approach`) and an `at_cap` (e.g. `propose`), click `[data-proposal-accept]`. (value ← proposal body; advance one step, §4.4.4/§4.4.7; `advance_target(needs_success, …)` = `needs_approach`, machine.py 40–48).
   289	5. **Copy.** Click `[data-copy]`. Read the clipboard via `page.evaluate("navigator.clipboard.readText()")` and assert it equals `client.get("/api/tickets/"+t_success+"/copy-text").text` byte-for-byte. **Fallback** (if headless clipboard read returns empty/blocked): assert the `[data-copy]` button text became "Copied" AND that the direct `copy-text` GET returns the expected block (title line + `state:` + `priority:` + field sections + `recap:` + `links:`), proving the data path even when the browser clipboard is unreadable.
   290	6. **Links add/remove.** In `[data-link-add]`: set `to_id` = `t_flaky`, kind = `relates`, click Add. Wait for flush; assert a `[data-link-row][data-link-kind="relates"]` appears whose text references `t_flaky`. Click that row's Remove; wait for flush; assert the `relates` row is gone.
   291	7. **Run history + event log.** goto `#/ticket/`+`t_impl`; assert `[data-run-history]` has exactly two `[data-run-row]`, one `[data-run-status="running"]` and one `[data-run-status="done"]`. Then (event log — asserted on `t_success`, the accepted ticket) goto `#/ticket/`+`t_success`; assert `[data-event-log]` contains a `[data-event-row][data-event-kind="ticket_created"]` and rows `[data-event-kind="proposal_accepted"]` and `[data-event-kind="state_changed"]` (the accept from item 4). (Runs live on `t_impl`; the accept events live on `t_success` — the two halves are asserted on their respective screens.)
   292	8. **Grant control.** On `t_success`: set `[data-grant-ceiling]` = `needs_plan`, `[data-grant-at-cap]` = `stop`, click `[data-grant-save]`. Wait for flush; assert the re-rendered `[data-grant-ceiling]` value is `needs_plan` and the `stop` radio is checked (grant persisted, `change_grant`).
   293	9. **State control + unblock + drop.** On `t_success`: set `[data-state-select]` = `needs_plan`, click `[data-state-jump]`; wait for flush; assert `[data-state="needs_plan"]`. Then goto `#/ticket/`+`t_flaky`; assert an auto-blocked marker (`[data-marker="auto-blocked"]`) and `[data-unblock]` present; click `[data-unblock]`; wait for flush; assert the auto-blocked marker is gone. Then goto `#/ticket/`+`t_success` (now `needs_plan`), click `[data-drop]`; wait for flush; goto `#/board`; assert no `[data-card][data-ticket-id=t_success]` in any column (it dropped out of the `needs_plan` column it sat in after the jump).
   294	10. **Chat.** goto `#/ticket/`+`t_impl` (a live ticket); assert `[data-chat]` present. Minimal exercise against the online echo fake: locate the first `textarea`/`input` inside `[data-chat]`, fill "smoke hello", click the panel's send control, wait for the text "echo: smoke hello" to appear inside `[data-chat]`. **Fallback** (T15 internal DOM differs / control not found): assert `[data-chat]` present AND `client.get("/api/chat/"+t_impl+"/status").json()["available"] is True` (proves the online path; chat/api.py 38–43, echo fake `available=True`).
   295	
   296	Print `SMOKE PASS (N checks)` and return 0.

   324	## 7. T15 interface assumptions (EXTERNAL CONTRACTS — reconcile against T15's landed code before/at integration)
   325	
   326	T15 owns D11 items 7 (proposal card) + 8 (grant-pair picker) + 10 (chat panel) and adds them to `Planner.components`. T16 calls them; it does not define them. The assumed signatures below let T16 be written now; when T15 lands, the diff to reality must be mechanical (rename a key / an attribute), not structural. If a mismatch is structural, that is an integration blocker to log, not to paper over.
   327	
   328	**`Planner.components.proposalCard(opts) → DOM node`** — assumed:
   329	- `opts.proposal` = the `fields[field].proposal` object `{body, proposed_by, created_at}`.
   330	- `opts.field` = the field name string (label / disambiguation).
   331	- `opts.requireGrant` = boolean. T16 passes `true` on every ticket-screen proposal card (mirrors Review; the grant-pair picker shows and Accept is gated on both halves per D11 #8). The accept endpoint always accepts the pair; on a non-gating field the server ignores it (resolution.py), so passing `true` uniformly is safe.
   332	- `opts.onAccept` = `function({edited_body, next_ceiling, at_cap}) → Promise`. `next_ceiling` already in wire form (a `TicketState` value or `"none"`); `at_cap` = `"stop"|"propose"`; `edited_body` = the quick-edit text or `null`/absent. T16 forwards these verbatim to `POST /api/tickets/{id}/accept/{field}`. Resolve on success (no manual re-render), reject with the `errorLine`-shaped error on failure.
   333	- Assumed internal attributes the smoke drives (item 3/4), to confirm against T15: grant-pair ceiling select `[data-grant-pick-ceiling]`, at-cap radios `[data-grant-pick-at-cap]`, Accept button `[data-proposal-accept]`, quick-edit textarea (optional). If T15 named them differently, update the smoke selectors only.
   334	
   335	**`Planner.components.chatPanel(opts) → DOM node`** — assumed:
   336	- `opts.entityId` = the ticket id (chat entity id = ticket id; §11). The panel self-manages: on mount it may `GET /api/chat/{entityId}/status`, and on send `POST /api/chat/{entityId}/send {text}` → `{reply_text, session_key}` (chat/api.py), appending the user line + reply locally (chat is interactive, not WS-flush-driven). Offline → renders a "gateway offline" notice (§11).
   337	- T16 wraps the returned node in `<div data-chat>`; the smoke's chat exercise (item 10) reaches into the panel generically with a resilient fallback because the panel's internal DOM is T15's.
   338

   344	- **`assets/app.js`, `assets/api.js`, `assets/config.js`, `assets/markdown.js`, `assets/tokens.css`** — not touched.  succeeded in 0ms:
    54	### 3.6 Links
    55	- One table: `links(from_id, to_id, kind)` with kind ∈ {`belongs_to` (ticket→sprint item), `parent_child` (ticket→ticket), `blocks` (ticket→ticket or ticket→sprint item), `relates` (any→any)}.
    56	- A ticket has at most one `belongs_to` link (enforced). Self-links rejected. `blocks` and `parent_child` cycles rejected at write time (transitive check).
    57	- A ticket is **blocked** iff it is the target of a `blocks` link whose source ticket is not `done` or `dropped`. Blocked tickets are ineligible for dispatch (Section 7.2).
    58	
    59	## 4. Ticket lifecycle and the write model
    60	
    61	### 4.1 States
    62	
    63	`needs_success → needs_approach → needs_plan → in_progress → needs_review → done`, plus `dropped` (terminal, reachable from any non-`done` state, human-only action).
    64	
    65	There is no stored "ready" or "satisfied" state. Derived views (Section 4.5) express them.
    66	
    67	### 4.2 Fields as structured objects
    68	
    69	`tickets.fields` is a JSON object with exactly four keys: `success`, `approach`, `plan`, `result`. Each is `{value: string|null, proposal: {body, proposed_by, created_at}|null, notes: string|null}`. `value` is canonical and only ever written by the resolution engine (Section 4.4). `notes` is free-guidance text ("when doing the approach, consider X"), writable directly by human or agent at any time (event-logged, no state effect).
    70	
    71	Each pre-terminal state has one **gating field**:
    72	
    73	| state | gating field | accepted proposal advances to |
    74	|---|---|---|
    75	| needs_success | success | needs_approach |
    76	| needs_approach | approach | needs_plan |
    77	| needs_plan | plan | in_progress |
    78	| in_progress | result | needs_review (or done, Section 4.4) |
    79	| needs_review | — (human approval of `result.value`) | done |
    80	
    81	The `result.notes` slot is the ticket's **review notes**: the specific things that need careful checking, written during planning and updated as review happens. Review itself is not a new mechanism: in `needs_review`, an agent orchestrates the Codex CLI against the result and these notes, fixes what review surfaces, and updates the notes — then the human approves. No extra states, entities, or flow machinery for this.
    82	
    83	### 4.3 Ceiling and at-cap
    84	
    85	- The agent grant is two things, always together: **how far** (`ceiling`) and **what happens there** (`at_cap`). These are the whole vocabulary of agent permission.
    86	- `ceiling` is the highest state an agent-caused advancement may move the ticket **into**. Below the ceiling, agents work freely: proposals on the current gating field auto-accept and advance (Section 4.4). Human actions ignore the ceiling entirely (the human may jump a ticket to any state, including straight to `done`; skipped gating fields simply keep `value = null`).
    87	- `at_cap` governs the ticket once it sits at its ceiling: `stop` — agents may not file any proposal; the ticket rests untouched until the human changes the grant. `propose` — agents may file a proposal on the current gating field only; it parks for the human; nothing advances.
    88	- Defaults at creation: `ceiling = needs_success`, `at_cap = propose` (an agent may draft a success condition for approval; nothing advances) [RULING R2 default].
    89	
    90	### 4.4 The resolution engine
    91	
    92	The only write path for `fields.*.value` and for agent-caused state changes. Semantics, exactly:
    93	
    94	1. An agent files a proposal on a field (CLI/API). At most one pending proposal per field: a new proposal replaces the pending one (replacement logged as `proposal_superseded`).
    95	2. If the field is the current state's gating field AND the advance-target state ≤ `ceiling`: the proposal auto-accepts — `value` ← proposal body, proposal cleared, state advances one step, events `proposal_accepted` + `state_changed` logged with `resolved_by: "auto"`.
    96	3. Otherwise the proposal stays pending. Pending proposals on the current gating field put the ticket in the human approval queue.
    97	4. Human resolution of a pending proposal: **accept** (value ← body), **edit** (value ← human-edited text; logged as accepted with `edited: true`), or leave it and discuss in chat. There is no reject action; a proposal can be superseded or the ticket dropped.
    98	5. Special case `in_progress`: an accepted `result` advances to `needs_review` unless `ceiling = done`, in which case it advances directly to `done`. Human approval in `needs_review` (a dedicated approve action, not a field proposal) sets `done`.
    99	6. Every transition writes exactly one `state_changed` event `{from, to, cause}`. One canonical function per transition edge in the code; no second writer.
   100	7. **The onward grant.** Human acceptance of a gating-field proposal (accept or edit-accept) requires the explicit grant pair: `next_ceiling` — any state at or beyond the newly entered state, or `none` meaning the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state — AND `at_cap` (`stop`: agents don't even propose from here; `propose`: agents may draft the next gating field for approval). Both are stated on every accept, even when the answer is "nothing further"; an accept without the pair is rejected with a structured error. Agent auto-accepts change neither ceiling nor at_cap. The `needs_review` approve action and sprint-item status accepts carry no onward grant (they are terminal for agent involvement).
   101	
   102	### 4.5 Derived views (must exist as queryable API endpoints, not stored columns)
   103	
   104	- **Approval queue**: tickets with a pending proposal on their current gating field (plus sprint items with pending status proposals, plus `needs_review` tickets). Ordered oldest-pending first.
   105	- **Pickup queue** (dispatcher eligibility): Section 7.2.
   106	- **Overdue**: tickets/items with `deadline` < current planning date and not `done`/`dropped`.
   107	
   108	## 5. Sprints — behavior rules
   109	
   110	- Creating a sprint with kickoff fields sets nothing frozen; an explicit `freeze-kickoff` action sets `kickoff_frozen_at`. Same pattern for review. Frozen-field writes are rejected with a structured error.

   183	## 9. HTTP API and events
   184	
   185	- REST JSON under `/api/`: CRUD + actions mirroring Section 8 verbs, PLUS the human actions that deliberately have no CLI form — accept/edit-accept with the grant pair, `needs_review` approve, unblock, grant changes, sprint create/freeze, day-plan accept/accept-all/invalidate/reject-all, drop — and `GET /api/board`, `GET /api/day/<date>`, `GET /api/sprint/current`, `GET /api/queues`. Every mutating endpoint routes through the same canonical functions (one writer per edge).
   186	- `WS /api/events?since=<id>`: tails the `events` table (poll interval 300ms server-side), pushing `{events:[...], cursor}` batches. Used by the UI as an **invalidation signal only** — the UI refetches JSON endpoints (debounced 250ms); it never reconciles event payloads into local state.
   187	- Test mode (Section 13): `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` and `POST /api/test/tick-dispatcher` run one tick synchronously; available only when test mode is on, else 404.
   188	
   189	## 10. The UI
   190	
   191	Six screens, served at `/`. Simplicity is the ruling aesthetic: fewer things done cleanly. Every screen renders from server JSON; refresh at any moment restores identical state.
   192	
   193	Design system (per PRINCIPLES.md, binding):
   194	- One token file, `assets/tokens.css`, owns everything themable: surfaces, text tokens, accent (bright/surface/text), radius scale, motion durations (fast/base/slow), spacing scale, border widths. The app's entire personality must be tunable by editing this one file. No new token categories without evidence of need.
   195	- Type: strict scale, five sizes maximum. Motion: entrances ease out, nothing bounces, all durations from motion tokens. Add nothing to a screen unless it makes the user feel something or a smart person genuinely needs it to understand the screen; no explanatory text for the obvious.
   196	- Before writing any component, record the component inventory for all five screens in decisions.md; build few, reuse hard, no near-duplicates.
   197	
   198	1. **Day (home)**: the brief (rendered markdown); the day-plan tree with per-node Accept / Invalidate and top-level Accept-all / Reject-all; the day's ticket list (ordered); a Review entry showing the pending count; and the day chat panel (Section 11) — quick capture happens here, by telling the planner, which files tickets/ideas via the CLI (Section 17).
   199	2. **Review**: the one-at-a-time review surface — one home of approvals (the Ticket screen is the other), and where the morning pass happens. Pending items — gating-field proposals, sprint-item status proposals, and `needs_review` results — presented one at a time, oldest first. Each shows the proposal rendered, a quick-edit textarea, Accept (with the mandatory grant-pair picker of Section 4.4: how far, and stop-vs-propose — plain pickers, including "no further"), Skip (item stays queued, move on), and a link to the full Ticket. Resolved items leave the queue immediately.
   200	3. **Board**: one column per ticket state (`dropped` hidden), cards show title, priority, deadline, project, pending-proposal marker, running-claim marker. No drag-and-drop in v1: card click opens Ticket.
   201	4. **Ticket**: all four fields as sections (value rendered as markdown; pending proposal shown with Accept/Edit, accepts carrying the onward grant; notes editable inline), recap, state control, ceiling and at-cap as plain pickers (no dial widgetry), a **Copy ticket** button (copies a plain-text block — title, state, priority, field values, recap, links — to the clipboard for pasting into any agent or editor), links, day/sprint assignment, run history, event log, and the chat panel (Section 11).
   202	5. **Sprint**: kickoff fields, items grouped by status (with ticket rollup counts and blockers-cleared flags), loose tickets, review fields (frozen states shown as locked).
   203	6. **Backlog & Ideas**: itemless sprint items list (by priority) and ideas list, each with create forms.
   204	
   205	## 11. Chat
   206	
   207	Each ticket and each day has at most one chat session. The chat panel connects through a **gateway adapter** wrapping the Hermes gateway (`tui_gateway.ws` import, same pattern as the old viewer). If the gateway is unreachable the panel renders "gateway offline" and the rest of the UI is unaffected. The entity's `chat_session_key` persists the session id. In tests the adapter is a fake that echoes. Live gateway behavior is a post-run human item, not an acceptance test.
   208	
   209	## 12. Seed and migration
   210	
   211	Migration from the markdown system is an in-run deliverable, not an afterthought. A frozen snapshot of the real planning data (taken 2026-07-04) is committed at `migration/source-snapshot/`; the build must migrate it successfully. The live directory is still never read at build/test time — the snapshot is the real-data target; final live cutover at switch time is the human's moment (§18.4).
   212	
   213	`plan seed --source <dir>` reads a markdown planning directory with the current system's shapes and imports: `sprints/current/sprint-kickoff.md` → sprint kickoff fields; `sprint-tracking.md` items → sprint items (statuses: Todo→todo, In Progress→active, Done→done, Blocked→blocked, Deferred→deferred_next_sprint; `Priority:`/`Urgency:`/`Project:` fields mapped; Mode dropped); `sprint-review.md` → review fields; the latest `daily/YYYY-MM-DD/workspace.md` tickets → tickets (Readiness mapping: Concepts→needs_success, Needs Shaping→needs_approach, Ready→needs_plan, In Progress→in_progress; `Ticket ID:` preserved as an alias field; `Chat ID:` → `chat_session_key`; `Priority:` mapped; body/success/approach text into the matching fields' values) linked to sprint items by title match when unambiguous, else standalone with sprint assignment; `deferred.md` → sprint items with `sprint_id = NULL` (project sections and P-labels mapped); `ideas.md` → ideas. `plan seed --demo` creates a deterministic demo dataset for dogfooding and manual testing: 8 tickets spanning every state with varied priorities, deadlines, and ceilings; one blocking link; one parent sprint item with children; one planned day. It requires an empty database and errors otherwise.

    76	Clk = Annotated[Clock, Depends(get_clock)]
    77	
    78	
    79	@contextmanager
    80	def txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    81	    """Wrap a NON-self-transacting writer (days/dispatch/links) so a mid-sequence
    82	    PlannerError rolls back cleanly. Never wrap a ticket/sprint writer — those open
    83	    their own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and would raise 'transaction within a transaction'."""
    84	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    85	    try:
    86	        yield
    87	    except BaseException:
    88	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    89	        raise
    90	    else:
    91	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    92	
    93	
    94	def parse_enum[E: StrEnum](enum_cls: type[E], raw: str, what: str) -> E:
    95	    try:
    96	        return enum_cls(raw)
    97	    except ValueError:
    98	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {what}", {what: raw}) from None
    99	
   100

   107	    deadline: str | None = None

   109	    sprint_id: str | None = None

   146	# --- grant marshallers ---------------------------------------------------------
   147	
   148	
   149	def _parse_next_ceiling(raw: str | None) -> NextCeiling | None:
   150	    if raw is None:
   151	        return None
   152	    if raw == NO_FURTHER:
   153	        return NO_FURTHER
   154	    try:
   155	        return TicketState(raw)
   156	    except ValueError:
   157	        raise PlannerError(
   158	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": raw}
   159	        ) from None
   160	
   161	
   162	def _parse_grant_at_cap(raw: str | None) -> AtCap | None:
   163	    if raw is None:
   164	        return None
   165	    try:
   166	        return AtCap(raw)
   167	    except ValueError:
   168	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": raw}) from None
   169	
   170	
   171	# --- private gap-fill writers (D2; A1: writer-shaped for relocation) ------------
   172	
   173	
   174	def _set_title(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, title: str, *,
   175	               title_max_chars: int, now: int) -> Ticket:
   176	    admission.validate_title(title, title_max_chars)
   177	    with txn(conn):
   178	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   179	        prev = ticket.title
   180	        conn.execute(
   181	            "UPDATE tickets SET title = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (title, now, ticket_id)
   182	        )
   183	        append_event(
   184	            conn,
   185	            ticket_id,
   186	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   187	            {"field": "title", "from": prev, "to": title},
   188	            now,
   189	        )
   190	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   191	
   192	
   193	def _set_project(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, project: Project | None, *,
   194	                 now: int) -> Ticket:
   195	    with txn(conn):
   196	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   197	        if ticket.sprint_item_id is not None:
   198	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is derived when parented")
   199	        prev = ticket.project.value if ticket.project is not None else None
   200	        new_value = project.value if project is not None else None
   201	        conn.execute(
   202	            "UPDATE tickets SET project = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   203	            (new_value, now, ticket_id),
   204	        )
   205	        append_event(
   206	            conn,
   207	            ticket_id,
   208	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   209	            {"field": "project", "from": prev, "to": new_value},
   210	            now,
   211	        )
   212	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)

   218	@router.post("/tickets")
   219	async def create_ticket(body: CreateTicketBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   220	                        clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   221	    now = clk.now_unix()
   222	    priority = parse_enum(Priority, body.priority, "priority") if body.priority is not None \
   223	        else Priority.P3
   224	    project = parse_enum(Project, body.project, "project") if body.project is not None else None
   225	    ticket = tickets_data.create_ticket(
   226	        conn,
   227	        title=body.title,
   228	        actor=ctx.actor,
   229	        now=now,
   230	        title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars,
   231	        project=project,
   232	        priority=priority,
   233	        deadline=body.deadline,
   234	        sprint_id=body.sprint_id,
   235	        sprint_item_id=body.sprint_item_id,
   236	    )
   237	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

   262	
   263	@router.patch("/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   264	async def patch_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   265	                       cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   266	    recognized = ("title", "priority", "deadline", "project", "sprint_id")
   267	    for key in body:
   268	        if key not in recognized:
   269	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown ticket field", {"field": key})
   270	    if not body:
   271	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no ticket fields to update", {})
   272	    now = clk.now_unix()
   273	    if "title" in body:
   274	        _set_title(conn, ticket_id, body["title"], title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars, now=now)
   275	    if "priority" in body:
   276	        priority = parse_enum(Priority, body["priority"], "priority")
   277	        tickets_data.set_priority(conn, ticket_id, priority=priority, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   278	    if "deadline" in body:
   279	        tickets_data.set_deadline(conn, ticket_id, deadline=body["deadline"], actor=ctx.actor,
   280	                                  now=now)
   281	    if "project" in body:
   282	        project = parse_enum(Project, body["project"], "project") \
   283	            if body["project"] is not None else None
   284	        _set_project(conn, ticket_id, project, now=now)
   285	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   286	        tickets_data.set_sprint(conn, ticket_id, sprint_id=body["sprint_id"], actor=ctx.actor,
   287	                                now=now)
   288	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id), now)
   289

   291	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/propose/{field}")
   292	async def propose_field(ticket_id: str, field: str, body: ProposeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   293	                        clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   294	    field_enum = parse_enum(FieldName, field, "field")
   295	    now = clk.now_unix()
   296	    if ctx.is_claimed_agent:
   297	        require_claim(conn, ctx, ticket_id, now)
   298	    ticket = tickets_data.file_proposal(
   299	        conn, ticket_id, field=field_enum, body=body.body, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   300	    )
   301	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

   304	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/accept/{field}")
   305	async def accept_field(ticket_id: str, field: str, body: AcceptBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   306	                       clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   307	    reject_agents(ctx)
   308	    field_enum = parse_enum(FieldName, field, "field")
   309	    now = clk.now_unix()

   319	        next_ceiling=next_ceiling,
   320	        at_cap=at_cap,
   321	    )
   322	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

   356	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/grant")
   357	async def grant_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: GrantBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   358	                       clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   359	    reject_agents(ctx)
   360	    now = clk.now_unix()
   361	    if body.ceiling is None or body.at_cap is None:
   362	        missing = [name for name, raw in (("ceiling", body.ceiling), ("at_cap", body.at_cap))
   363	                   if raw is None]
   364	        raise PlannerError(
   365	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "grant requires ceiling and at_cap", {"missing": missing}
   366	        )
   367	    try:
   368	        ceiling = TicketState(body.ceiling)
   369	    except ValueError:
   370	        raise PlannerError(
   371	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown ceiling", {"ceiling": body.ceiling}
   372	        ) from None
   373	    try:
   374	        at_cap = AtCap(body.at_cap)
   375	    except ValueError:
   376	        raise PlannerError(
   377	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": body.at_cap}
   378	        ) from None
   379	    ticket = tickets_data.change_grant(
   380	        conn, ticket_id, ceiling=ceiling, at_cap=at_cap, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   381	    )
   382	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)
    63	
    64	
    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )
    91	
    92	
    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:
    94	    plan = days_data.load_plan(conn, did)
    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})
    97	    return plan
    98	
    99	
   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})

   109	async def get_day(date: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:

   114	@router.patch("/day/{date}")
   115	async def patch_day(date: str, body: DayPatchBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   116	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   117	    now = clk.now_unix()
   118	    if body.brief is None and body.notes is None:
   119	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no day fields to update", {})
   120	    if body.brief is not None:
   121	        with txn(conn):
   122	            days_data.set_brief(conn, did, body.brief, now)
   123	    if body.notes is not None:

   125	            days_data.set_notes(conn, did, body.notes, now)
   126	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   141	async def remove_day_ticket(date: str, ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg,
   142	                            clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   143	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   144	    now = clk.now_unix()
   145	    with txn(conn):
   146	        days_data.remove_day_ticket(conn, did, ticket_id, now)
   147	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   150	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept")
   151	async def plan_accept(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   152	                      clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   153	    reject_agents(ctx)
   154	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   155	    now = clk.now_unix()
   156	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   157	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   158	    if isinstance(node, int):

   161	    with txn(conn):
   162	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   163	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   180	                          clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   181	    reject_agents(ctx)
   182	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   183	    now = clk.now_unix()
   184	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   185	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   186	    if isinstance(node, int):
   187	        _require_child(tree, node)
   188	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_child(tree, node)
   189	    else:
   190	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_root(tree)
   191	    with txn(conn):
   192	        replans = days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   193	    for request in replans:  # post-commit enqueue: the R5 consumer sees committed state only
   194	        submit_replan(did, request)
   195	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   204	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.reject_all(tree)

   206	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   207	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

    91	def list_tickets(
    92	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    93	    now: int,
    94	    *,

    96	    project: Project | None,
    97	    sprint_id: str | None,

    99	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   100	    clauses: list[str] = []

   103	        clauses.append("state = ?")

   116	        "SELECT id FROM tickets" + where + " ORDER BY created_at ASC, id", tuple(params)
   117	    ).fetchall()

   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)
   124	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   125	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   126	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",

   129	    day_rows = conn.execute(
   130	        "SELECT day_id FROM day_tickets WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY day_id ASC", (ticket_id,)
   131	    ).fetchall()
   132	    total = int(
   133	        conn.execute(
   134	            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ?", (ticket_id,)
   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]
   136	    )
   137	    running = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT 1 FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? AND status = 'running' LIMIT 1", (ticket_id,)
   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(
   141	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(
   145	        {
   146	            "blocked": core_links.is_blocked(conn, ticket_id),
   147	            "effective_sprint_id": tickets_data.get_effective_sprint_id(conn, ticket_id),
   148	            "links": [
   149	                {"from_id": str(r["from_id"]), "to_id": str(r["to_id"]), "kind": str(r["kind"])}
   150	                for r in link_rows
   151	            ],
   152	            "day_ids": [str(r["day_id"]) for r in day_rows],
   153	            "run_summary": {
   154	                "total": total,
   155	                "running": running is not None,
   156	                "latest": run_json(latest) if latest is not None else None,
   157	            },
   158	        }
   159	    )
   160	    return detail

   186	    fields = ticket.fields
   187

   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )

   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}

   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()

   275	    ticket_title: dict[str, str] = {}

   285	            }

   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.
   298	    for entry in digest:
   299	        if entry["kind"] == "review":

   302	                "SELECT created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? AND kind = 'state_changed' "
   303	                "AND json_extract(payload, '$.to') = 'needs_review' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],

    77	# --- private gap-fill writers (D1/D3; A1: writer-shaped for relocation) ---------
    78	
    79	
    80	def _create_idea(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, body: str, project: Project | None,
    81	                 now: int) -> JsonDict:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "idea title is required", {})
    84	    idea_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["idea"])
    85	    with txn(conn):
    86	        conn.execute(
    87	            "INSERT INTO ideas (id, title, body, project, created_at, updated_at) "
    88	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    89	            (idea_id, title, body, project.value if project is not None else None, now, now),
    90	        )
    91	        append_event(conn, idea_id, EventKind.idea_created, {"title": title, "source": "api"}, now)
    92	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM ideas WHERE id = ?", (idea_id,)).fetchone()
    93	    assert row is not None
    94	    return sprints_views.idea_json(row)

    97	def _set_sprint_dates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, date_start: str | None,
    98	                      date_end: str | None, clock: Clock) -> None:
    99	    sprint = sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   100	    new_start = date_start if date_start is not None else sprint.date_start
   101	    new_end = date_end if date_end is not None else sprint.date_end
   102	    for label, value in (("date_start", new_start), ("date_end", new_end)):
   103	        try:
   104	            date.fromisoformat(value)
   105	        except ValueError as exc:
   106	            raise PlannerError(
   107	                ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {label}", {label: value}
   108	            ) from exc
   109	    if new_start > new_end:
   110	        raise PlannerError(
   111	            ErrorCode.validation,
   112	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   113	            {"date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   114	        )
   115	    others = [
   116	        DateRange(id=str(r["id"]), date_start=str(r["date_start"]), date_end=str(r["date_end"]))
   117	        for r in conn.execute(
   118	            "SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints WHERE id != ?", (sprint_id,)
   119	        ).fetchall()
   120	    ]
   121	    conflict = find_overlap(new_start, new_end, others)
   122	    if conflict is not None:
   123	        raise PlannerError(
   124	            ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   125	            "sprint dates overlap",
   126	            {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   127	        )
   128	    now = clock.now_unix()
   129	    with txn(conn):
   130	        conn.execute(
   131	            "UPDATE sprints SET date_start = ?, date_end = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   132	            (new_start, new_end, now, sprint_id),
   133	        )
   134	        if new_start != sprint.date_start:
   135	            append_event(
   136	                conn,
   137	                sprint_id,
   138	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   139	                {"field": "date_start", "from": sprint.date_start, "to": new_start},
   140	                now,
   141	            )
   142	        if new_end != sprint.date_end:
   143	            append_event(
   144	                conn,
   145	                sprint_id,
   146	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   147	                {"field": "date_end", "from": sprint.date_end, "to": new_end},
   148	                now,
   149	            )
   150	
   151	
   152	def _marshal_item_deadline(raw: object) -> None:
   153	    if raw is None:
   154	        return
   155	    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   156	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw})
   157	    try:
   158	        date.fromisoformat(raw)
   159	    except ValueError as exc:
   160	        raise PlannerError(
   161	            ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw}
   162	        ) from exc
   163

   207	@router.patch("/items/{item_id}")
   208	async def patch_item(item_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   209	                     clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   210	    recognized = set(_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS) | {"sprint_id", "status", "blocked_by"}
   211	    for key in body:
   212	        if key not in recognized:
   213	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown item field", {"field": key})
   214	    if not body:
   215	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no item fields to update", {})
   216	    if "blocked_by" in body and "status" not in body:
   217	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "blocked_by requires status", {})
   218	    for field in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   219	        if field in body:
   220	            if field == "deadline":
   221	                _marshal_item_deadline(body["deadline"])
   222	            sprints_data.update_item_field(conn, item_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   223	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   224	        sprints_data.assign_item_sprint(conn, item_id, body["sprint_id"], clock=clk)
   225	    if "status" in body:
   226	        to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body["status"], "status")
   227	        sprints_data.transition_item_status(
   228	            conn, item_id, to_status, clock=clk, by_agent=not ctx.is_human,
   229	            blocked_by=body.get("blocked_by"),
   230	        )
   231	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)

     1	"""§7.6 request context and write validation. This is the server-side dependency
     2	that T10/T13 route bodies consume: it reads the X-Plan-* headers, classifies each
     3	request as a claimed agent, a plain agent, or the human, and validates a presented
     4	claim against a ticket's active lease. Lease liveness is decided only by
     5	dispatch.logic.claims — never re-derived here.
     6	
     7	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py
     8	family), not a logic or data module."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import sqlite3
    13	from dataclasses import dataclass
    14	from typing import Final, NoReturn
    15	
    16	from fastapi import Request
    17	
    18	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    19	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    20	from planner.dispatch.logic.claims import has_active_claim, is_expired
    21	
    22	X_PLAN_RUN_ID: Final = "X-Plan-Run-Id"
    23	X_PLAN_CLAIM: Final = "X-Plan-Claim"
    24	X_PLAN_ACTOR: Final = "X-Plan-Actor"
    25	_DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR: Final = "agent"    # §7.6: PLAN_ACTOR default "agent"
    26	_HUMAN_ACTOR: Final = "human"
    27	
    28	
    29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30	class RequestContext:
    31	    actor: str
    32	    run_id: str | None
    33	    claim: str | None
    34	    is_claimed_agent: bool
    35	    is_human: bool
    36	
    37	
    38	def _normalize(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
    39	    """strip(); an empty or whitespace-only header is an absent header."""
    40	    if raw is None:
    41	        return None
    42	    stripped = raw.strip()
    43	    return stripped or None
    44	
    45	
    46	def _classify(run_id: str | None, claim: str | None, actor: str | None) -> RequestContext:
    47	    """Pure classification over the §1.1 truth table; inputs are pre-normalized."""
    48	    if run_id is not None or claim is not None:
    49	        # Any claim header present (whole or half) is a claimed-agent attempt.
    50	        return RequestContext(
    51	            actor=actor if actor is not None else _DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR,
    52	            run_id=run_id,
    53	            claim=claim,
    54	            is_claimed_agent=True,
    55	            is_human=False,
    56	        )
    57	    if actor is not None:
    58	        # Non-dispatched agent context (planner-main chat agent): proposer from PLAN_ACTOR.
    59	        return RequestContext(
    60	            actor=actor,
    61	            run_id=None,
    62	            claim=None,
    63	            is_claimed_agent=False,
    64	            is_human=False,
    65	        )
    66	    # The human: UI requests carry no agent headers.
    67	    return RequestContext(
    68	        actor=_HUMAN_ACTOR,
    69	        run_id=None,
    70	        claim=None,
    71	        is_claimed_agent=False,
    72	        is_human=True,
    73	    )
    74	
    75	
    76	def request_context(request: Request) -> RequestContext:
    77	    """FastAPI dependency: reads the three headers, normalizes, classifies. No DB, no clock."""
    78	    headers = request.headers
    79	    return _classify(
    80	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_RUN_ID)),
    81	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_CLAIM)),
    82	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_ACTOR)),
    83	    )
    84	
    85	
    86	def _reject_stale(
    87	    reason: str,
    88	    message: str,
    89	    ctx: RequestContext,
    90	    ticket_id: str,
    91	    active_claim_present: bool,
    92	    stored_lock: str | None,
    93	    extra: JsonDict | None = None,
    94	) -> NoReturn:
    95	    # Presented values are echoed back — except a presented token equal to the stored
    96	    # claim_lock (the expired/run_mismatch/missing-half paths reach here with a correct
    97	    # token): verbatim it would put the live stored token into a detail that may be
    98	    # logged or displayed, so it is redacted.
    99	    presented_claim = ctx.claim
   100	    if presented_claim is not None and presented_claim == stored_lock:
   101	        presented_claim = "(redacted)"
   102	    detail: JsonDict = {
   103	        "reason": reason,
   104	        "ticket_id": ticket_id,
   105	        "presented_run_id": ctx.run_id,
   106	        "presented_claim": presented_claim,
   107	        "active_claim_present": active_claim_present,
   108	    }
   109	    if extra is not None:
   110	        detail.update(extra)
   111	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.stale_claim, message, detail)
   112	
   113	
   114	def require_claim(
   115	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ctx: RequestContext, ticket_id: str, now: int
   116	) -> None:
   117	    """§7.6 write validation. Checks run in the fixed order (ticket exists → active
   118	    claim → unexpired → token → run id); the first failure raises stale_claim naming
   119	    which check failed. The stored token is never echoed. now is unix seconds."""
   120	    row = conn.execute(
   121	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   122	    ).fetchone()
   123	    if row is None:
   124	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
   125	    claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   126	    claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   127	    active = has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now)
   128	
   129	    if ctx.run_id is None or ctx.claim is None:
   130	        missing = [
   131	            name
   132	            for name, value in ((X_PLAN_RUN_ID, ctx.run_id), (X_PLAN_CLAIM, ctx.claim))
   133	            if value is None
   134	        ]
   135	        _reject_stale(
   136	            "missing_header",
   137	            "claim headers incomplete",
   138	            ctx,
   139	            ticket_id,
   140	            active,

    70	  consecutive_failures INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- §7.5
    71	  chat_session_key     TEXT,
    72	  alias                TEXT,                         -- migration "Ticket ID:" (§12)
    73	  fields               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"success":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"approach":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"plan":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"result":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null}}',
    74	  claim_lock           TEXT,                         -- §7.3 claim token; NULL = unclaimed
    75	  claim_expires        INTEGER,
    76	  created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL,
    77	  updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL
    78	);
    79	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tickets_alias ON tickets(alias) WHERE alias IS NOT NULL;
    80	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tickets_state ON tickets(state);
    81	
    82	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS days (
    83	  id               TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- day_YYYY-MM-DD (planning date, §3.4)
    84	  brief            TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    85	  notes            TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    86	  plan             TEXT,                             -- JSON PlanTree | NULL
    87	  chat_session_key TEXT,
    88	  created_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
    89	  updated_at       INTEGER NOT NULL
    90	);
    91	
    92	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS day_tickets (
    93	  day_id    TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES days(id),
    94	  ticket_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id),
    95	  position  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (position >= 0),  -- contiguous from 0, writer-enforced
    96	  PRIMARY KEY (day_id, ticket_id)
    97	);
    98	
    99	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ideas (
   100	  id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- idea_<slug>
   101	  title      TEXT NOT NULL,
   102	  body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
   103	  project    TEXT CHECK (project IN ('Vylo','Tribe','Learning','Other')),  -- nullable (§3.5)
   104	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   105	  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
   106	);
   107	
   108	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (                   -- §3.6, exactly the spec's three columns
   109	  from_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   110	  to_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
   111	  kind    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('belongs_to','parent_child','blocks','relates')),
   112	  PRIMARY KEY (from_id, to_id, kind),
   113	  CHECK (from_id <> to_id)
   114	);
   115	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_links_one_belongs_to
   116	  ON links(from_id) WHERE kind = 'belongs_to';       -- at most one belongs_to per ticket
   117	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_links_to ON links(to_id, kind);
   118	
   119	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (                  -- §3, append-only
   120	  id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   121	  entity_id  TEXT NOT NULL,
   122	  kind       TEXT NOT NULL,
   123	  payload    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',             -- JSON
   124	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
   125	);
   126	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_entity ON events(entity_id, id);
   127	
   128	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (                    -- §7.3
   129	  id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- run_<slug>
   130	  ticket_id  TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id),
   131	  status     TEXT NOT NULL
   132	             CHECK (status IN ('running','done','blocked','crashed','timed_out','reclaimed','spawn_failed')),
   133	  started_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   134	  ended_at   INTEGER,
   135	  summary    TEXT,
   136	  error      TEXT,
   137	  pid        INTEGER
   138	);
   139	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_ticket ON runs(ticket_id, started_at);
   140	
   141	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS boundary_runs (           -- §6.2: never twice per date
   142	  planning_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                    -- ISO date
   143	  ran_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
   144	  judgment      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (judgment IN ('ok','skipped','failed'))
   145	);
   146	"""
   147	
   148	
   149	def connect(db_path: str, busy_timeout_ms: int = 5000) -> sqlite3.Connection:
   150	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, isolation_level=None)
   151	    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   152	    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
   153	    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
   154	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA busy_timeout={int(busy_timeout_ms)}")
   155	    return conn
   156	
   157	
   158	def create_schema(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
   159	    conn.executescript(DDL)
   160	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version={SCHEMA_VERSION}")

    18	_FIRST_FAKE_PID = 90001

    20	
    21	@dataclass
    22	class FakeSpawnAdapter:
    23	    calls: list[SpawnRequest] = field(default_factory=list)
    24	    results: list[SpawnResult] = field(default_factory=list)  # scripted, popped FIFO
    25	    next_pid: int = _FIRST_FAKE_PID
    26	
    27	    def spawn(self, request: SpawnRequest) -> SpawnResult:
    28	        self.calls.append(request)
    29	        if self.results:
    30	            return self.results.pop(0)
    31	        pid = self.next_pid
    32	        self.next_pid += 1
    33	        return SpawnResult(ok=True, pid=pid)
    34	
    35	
    36	@dataclass
    37	class FakeBoundaryAdapter:
    38	    judgment_result: BoundaryJudgment | None = None
    39	    replan_tree: PlanTree | None = None
    40	    replan_child_node: PlanNode | None = None
    41	    fail: bool = False
    42	    calls: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # method names, in order
    43	
    44	    def judgment(self, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> BoundaryJudgment:
    45	        self.calls.append("judgment")
    46	        if self.fail:
    47	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    48	        if self.judgment_result is not None:
    49	            return self.judgment_result
    50	        children = [
    51	            PlanNode(
    52	                ticket_id=entry.get("id"),
    53	                note=str(entry.get("title", "")),
    54	                status=NodeStatus.proposed,
    55	                position=index,
    56	            )
    57	            for index, entry in enumerate(inputs.carryover)
    58	        ]
    59	        tree = PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake focus"), children=children)
    60	        return BoundaryJudgment(
    61	            brief_markdown=f"# Brief for {inputs.planning_date}", plan_tree=tree
    62	        )
    63	
    64	    def replan_root(self, day_id: str, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanTree:
    65	        self.calls.append("replan_root")
    66	        if self.fail:
    67	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    68	        if self.replan_tree is not None:
    69	            return self.replan_tree
    70	        return PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake replanned focus"), children=[])
    71	
    72	    def replan_child(self, day_id: str, child: PlanNode, inputs: BoundaryInputs) -> PlanNode:
    73	        self.calls.append("replan_child")
    74	        if self.fail:
    75	            raise RuntimeError("fake boundary failure")
    76	        if self.replan_child_node is not None:
    77	            return self.replan_child_node
    78	        return PlanNode(
    79	            ticket_id=child.ticket_id,
    80	            note="Fake replanned child",
    81	            status=NodeStatus.proposed,
    82	            position=child.position,
    83	        )

    85

    87	class EchoGatewayAdapter:
