    89	    next_session: int = 1
    90	
    91	    def status(self) -> GatewayStatus:
    92	        return GatewayStatus(available=True)
    93	
    94	    def send(self, session_key: str | None, entity_id: str, text: str) -> ChatSendResult:
    95	        self.calls.append((session_key, entity_id, text))
    96	        if session_key is None:
    97	            session_key = f"fake-sess-{self.next_session}"
    98	            self.next_session += 1
    99	        return ChatSendResult(reply_text=f"echo: {text}", session_key=session_key)

     1	"""§4.4 resolution semantics as pure Decisions. _accept_gating_proposal is the
     2	sole constructor of any Decision that traverses the gating-acceptance edges 1-5
     3	(auto and human alike); _state_change is the sole constructor of state_changed
     4	EventSpecs. Together with data._apply_decision (the sole appender) this is the
     5	"one canonical writer per transition" guarantee (§4.4.6)."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    14	    AtCap,
    15	    FieldName,
    16	    FieldSlot,
    17	    GrantPair,
    18	    NextCeiling,
    19	    Proposal,
    20	    Ticket,
    21	    TicketState,
    22	)
    23	from planner.tickets.logic import admission, fields_codec, machine
    24	from planner.tickets.logic.decisions import Decision, EventSpec
    25	
    26	CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "auto_accept"
    27	CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "human_accept"
    28	CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE: Final[str] = "review_approve"
    29	CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP: Final[str] = "human_state_jump"
    30	CAUSE_DROP: Final[str] = "drop"
    31	CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT: Final[str] = "onward_grant"
    32	CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT: Final[str] = "human_grant"
    33	RESOLVED_BY_AUTO: Final[str] = "auto"
    34	RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN: Final[str] = "human"
    35	
    36	
    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Adapter registry (§9.4). Selects real vs fake (vs offline gateway) per adapter
     2	from config keys. `auto` resolves to fake under test mode and real otherwise;
     3	explicit values always win. An unknown value fails loudly at startup."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass
     8	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9	
    10	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, GatewayAdapter, SpawnAdapter
    11	from planner.core.adapters.fakes import (
    12	    EchoGatewayAdapter,
    13	    FakeBoundaryAdapter,
    14	    FakeSpawnAdapter,
    15	    OfflineGatewayAdapter,
    16	)
    17	from planner.core.adapters.real import (
    18	    RealBoundaryAdapter,
    19	    RealGatewayAdapter,
    20	    RealSpawnAdapter,
    21	)
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	
    24	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    25	    from planner.core.config import Config
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29	class Adapters:
    30	    spawn: SpawnAdapter
    31	    boundary: BoundaryAdapter
    32	    gateway: GatewayAdapter
    33	
    34	
    35	def _resolve_auto(choice: str, test_mode: bool) -> str:
    36	    if choice == "auto":
    37	        return "fake" if test_mode else "real"
    38	    return choice
    39	
    40	
    41	def _select_spawn(config: Config) -> SpawnAdapter:
    42	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.spawn_adapter, config.test_mode)
    43	    if choice == "fake":
    44	        return FakeSpawnAdapter()
    45	    if choice == "real":
    46	        return RealSpawnAdapter(config)
    47	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown spawn_adapter: {config.spawn_adapter!r}")
    48	
    49	
    50	def _select_boundary(config: Config) -> BoundaryAdapter:
    51	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.boundary_adapter, config.test_mode)
    52	    if choice == "fake":
    53	        return FakeBoundaryAdapter()
    54	    if choice == "real":
    55	        return RealBoundaryAdapter(config)
    56	    raise PlannerError(
    57	        ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown boundary_adapter: {config.boundary_adapter!r}"
    58	    )
    59	
    60	
    61	def _select_gateway(config: Config) -> GatewayAdapter:
    62	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.gateway_adapter, config.test_mode)
    63	    if choice == "fake":
    64	        return EchoGatewayAdapter()
    65	    if choice == "offline":
    66	        return OfflineGatewayAdapter()
    67	    if choice == "real":
    68	        return RealGatewayAdapter(config)
    69	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown gateway_adapter: {config.gateway_adapter!r}")
    70	
    71	
    72	def build_adapters(config: Config) -> Adapters:
    73	    return Adapters(
    74	        spawn=_select_spawn(config),
    75	        boundary=_select_boundary(config),
    76	        gateway=_select_gateway(config),
    77	    )

    17	_TABLES = (
    18	    "sprints", "sprint_items", "tickets", "days", "day_tickets",

    20	)

    23	def seed_demo(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    24	    for table in _TABLES:
    25	        if conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}").fetchone()[0] > 0:
    26	            raise PlannerError(
    27	                ErrorCode.db_not_empty, "seed --demo requires an empty database", {"table": table}
    28	            )
    29	    now = int(time.time())
    30	    today = datetime.now().astimezone().date()
    31	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    32	    try:
    33	        _build_demo(conn, now, today)
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	    except Exception:
    36	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    37	        raise
    38

   115	
   116	def _insert_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date) -> str:
   117	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])
   118	    date_start = (today - timedelta(days=3)).isoformat()
   119	    date_end = (today + timedelta(days=10)).isoformat()
   120	    conn.execute(
   121	        "INSERT INTO sprints (id, name, date_start, date_end, limiting_factor, primary_bet, "
   122	        "supports, premortem, created_at, updated_at) "
   123	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   124	        (
   125	            sprint_id, "Demo Sprint", date_start, date_end,
   126	            "Demo: one clear slice must ship.",
   127	            "Demo: shipping the feature slice unblocks the review queue.",
   128	            "- Demo support line.", "- Demo premortem line.", now, now,
   129	        ),
   130	    )
   131	    append_event(
   132	        conn, sprint_id, EventKind.sprint_created,
   133	        {"name": "Demo Sprint", "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end, "source": "demo"},
   134	        now,
   135	    )
   136	    return sprint_id
   137	
   138	
   139	def _insert_item(
   140	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   141	    sprint_id: str,
   142	    title: str,
   143	    status: str,
   144	    priority: str,
   145	    project: str,
   146	    body: str,
   147	    now: int,
   148	) -> str:
   149	    item_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint_item"])
   150	    conn.execute(
   151	        "INSERT INTO sprint_items (id, title, body, status, priority, deadline, project, "
   152	        "current_state_note, sprint_id, created_at, updated_at) "
   153	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   154	        (item_id, title, body, status, priority, None, project, "", sprint_id, now, now),
   155	    )
   156	    append_event(
   157	        conn, item_id, EventKind.sprint_item_created,
   158	        {"title": title, "status": status, "sprint_id": sprint_id, "source": "demo"},
   159	        now,
   160	    )
   161	    return item_id
   162	
   163	
   164	def _fields(title: str, keys: tuple[str, ...], result_notes: str | None = None) -> str:
   165	    slots: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {
   166	        name: {"value": None, "proposal": None, "notes": None}
   167	        for name in ("success", "approach", "plan", "result")
   168	    }
   169	    for key in keys:
   170	        slots[key]["value"] = f"Demo {key} for {title}"
   171	    if result_notes is not None:
   172	        slots["result"]["notes"] = result_notes
   173	    return json.dumps(slots)
   174	
   175	
   176	def _insert_ticket(
   177	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   178	    now: int,
   179	    *,
   180	    title: str,
   181	    state: str,
   182	    priority: str,
   183	    deadline: str | None,
   184	    ceiling: str,
   185	    at_cap: str,
   186	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
   187	    sprint_id: str | None,
   188	    project: str | None,
   189	    fields: str,
   190	) -> str:
   191	    ticket_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["ticket"])
   192	    conn.execute(
   193	        "INSERT INTO tickets (id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, "
   194	        "sprint_id, recap, ceiling, at_cap, auto_blocked, consecutive_failures, "
   195	        "chat_session_key, alias, fields, created_at, updated_at) "
   196	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   197	        (
   198	            ticket_id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, sprint_id,
   199	            "", ceiling, at_cap, 0, 0, None, None, fields, now, now,
   200	        ),
   201	    )
   202	    append_event(
   203	        conn, ticket_id, EventKind.ticket_created,
   204	        {"title": title, "state": state, "alias": None, "source": "demo"},
   205	        now,
   206	    )
   207	    return ticket_id
   208	
   209	
   210	def _insert_link(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: str) -> None:
   211	    conn.execute(
   212	        "INSERT INTO links (from_id, to_id, kind) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (from_id, to_id, kind)
   213	    )
   214	    append_event(
   215	        conn, from_id, EventKind.link_added,
   216	        {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind, "source": "demo"},
   217	        now,
   218	    )

   221	def _insert_day(
   222	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date, t4: str, t8: str, t5: str
   223	) -> None:
   224	    did = day_id(today)
   225	    plan_tree = {
   226	        "root": {"focus": "Ship the demo feature slice.", "status": "accepted"},
   227	        "children": [
   228	            {
   229	                "ticket_id": t4, "note": "Land the slice behind the flag.",
   230	                "status": "accepted", "position": 0,
   231	            },
   232	            {
   233	                "ticket_id": t8, "note": "Stabilise the login test before review.",
   234	                "status": "proposed", "position": 1,
   235	            },
   236	        ],
   237	    }
   238	    conn.execute(
   239	        "INSERT INTO days (id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at) "
   240	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   241	        (
   242	            did, "Demo day: ship the feature slice and clear the review queue.", "",
   243	            json.dumps(plan_tree), None, now, now,
   244	        ),
   245	    )
   246	    append_event(conn, did, EventKind.day_created, {"source": "demo"}, now)
   247	    for ticket_id, position in ((t4, 0), (t8, 1), (t5, 2)):
   248	        conn.execute(
   249	            "INSERT INTO day_tickets (day_id, ticket_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   250	            (did, ticket_id, position),
   251	        )
   252	        append_event(
   253	            conn, did, EventKind.day_ticket_added,
   254	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "position": position, "cause": "demo", "source": "demo"},
   255	            now,
   256	        )

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""T14 UI-foundation self-smoke. Boots an in-process test-mode server (uvicorn on a
     3	daemon thread, temp DB, fake clock) and drives real headless chromium (Playwright) to
     4	prove the shell references and their order, the meta keys the JS consumes, the default
     5	route + placeholders wired through the router, and the WS event-append -> one-debounced
     6	-flush invariant (one event => exactly one flush; two rapid events => still one flush).
     7	Straight-line, assert-based, numbered `ok NN` prints; any failed assertion raises ->
     8	traceback + non-zero exit; the try/finally always stops the server, closes the browser,
     9	and removes the temp dir. Exits 0 with a final SMOKE PASS line on success. Not under
    10	tests/ — never touched by pytest. Run from the repo root:
    11	
    12	    cd /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2 && .venv/bin/python \

    61	def free_or(preferred: int) -> int:
    62	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as probe:
    63	        try:
    64	            probe.bind(("127.0.0.1", preferred))
    65	            return preferred
    66	        except OSError:
    67	            pass
    68	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as fallback:
    69	        fallback.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    70	        return int(fallback.getsockname()[1])
    71	
    72	
    73	def wait_ready(base: str, budget: float = 15.0, interval: float = 0.25) -> None:
    74	    deadline = time.time() + budget
    75	    while time.time() < deadline:
    76	        try:
    77	            if httpx.get(f"{base}/api/meta", timeout=1.0).status_code == 200:
    78	                return
    79	        except httpx.HTTPError:
    80	            pass
    81	        time.sleep(interval)
    82	    raise AssertionError("server did not become ready within budget")
    83

    85	def main() -> int:
    86	    os.chdir(REPO)  # create_app mounts StaticFiles(directory="assets") cwd-relative
    87	    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="t14-smoke-"))
    88	    port = free_or(PORT)
    89	    base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    90	    env = {
    91	        "PLAN_TEST_MODE": "1",

    93	        "PLAN_DB_PATH": str(tmp / "planning.db"),
    94	        "PLAN_PORT": str(port),

    96	        "PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH": str(tmp / "dispatcher.lock"),

    98	    }
    99	    config = load_config(path=None, env=env)
   100	
   101	    (tmp / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   102	    with connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms) as bootstrap:
   103	        create_schema(bootstrap)
   104	    clock = build_clock(config)

   106

   113	    )

     1	/* Hash router, screen registry, and boot. Owns the shell instance. The router
     2	 * exposes exactly Planner.registerScreen(name, render) — screens navigate via
     3	 * anchors and read params; no navigate(), no route events, no current-route
     4	 * getter. Every render derives from location.hash plus fresh fetches, so refresh
     5	 * restores state by construction. Classic script: attaches Planner.registerScreen. */

     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	  var config = Planner.config;
    10	
    11	  var registry = {};
    12	  var shell = null;
    13	  var lastHash = null;
    14	
    15	  Planner.registerScreen = function (name, render) {
    16	    registry[name] = { render: render };
    17	  };
    18	
    19	  function quietLine(text) {
    20	    var div = document.createElement("div");
    21	    div.className = "quiet-line";
    22	    div.textContent = text;
    23	    return div;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  // Placeholders — T15–T17 scripts, appended after this file, re-register the real

    32	  });
    33	
    34	  function route() {
    35	    var hash = location.hash;
    36	    if (hash === "" || hash === "#") {
    37	      location.replace(config.ROUTES.day);
    38	      return;
    39	    }
    40	    var segments = hash.slice(1).split("/").filter(Boolean);
    41	    var name = segments[0];
    42	    var params = {};
    43	    if (name === "ticket") {
    44	      params.id = segments[1];
    45	    }
    46	    var known = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, name);
    47	    // Exact route shapes only: #/<screen> or #/ticket/<id> — no trailing extras.
    48	    var badShape = name === "ticket"
    49	      ? segments.length !== 2 || !params.id
    50	      : segments.length !== 1;
    51	    if (!known || badShape) {
    52	      shell.setActiveNav(null);
    53	      shell.content.replaceChildren(quietLine("no such screen"));
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	    shell.setActiveNav(name);
    57	    var entrance = hash !== lastHash;
    58	    lastHash = hash;
    59	    var screenDiv = document.createElement("div");
    60	    screenDiv.className = entrance ? "screen screen-enter" : "screen";
    61	    shell.content.replaceChildren(screenDiv);
    62	    registry[name].render(screenDiv, params);
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  function refreshBadge() {
    66	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.QUEUES).then(
    67	      function (res) {
    68	        shell.setReviewBadge((res.approvals || []).length);
    69	      },
    70	      function () {
    71	        console.debug("[planner] queues fetch failed; badge unchanged");
    72	      }
    73	    );
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  function onFlush() {
    77	    refreshBadge();
    78	    route();
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  function boot() {
    82	    shell = Planner.components.appShell();
    83	    document.getElementById("app").appendChild(shell);
    84	
    85	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.META).then(
    86	      function (meta) {
    87	        return meta.ui_debounce_ms;
    88	      },
    89	      function () {
    90	        console.debug("[planner] meta fetch failed; using default debounce");
    91	        return config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    92	      }
    93	    ).then(function (debounceMs) {
    94	      Planner.bus.onInvalidate(onFlush);
    95	      Planner.bus.start(debounceMs);
    96	      window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
    97	      refreshBadge();
    98	      route();
    99	    });
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
   103	    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
   104	  } else {
   105	    boot();
   106	  }
   107	})();

     9	  function make(tag, className, text) {
    10	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    11	    if (className) {
    12	      node.className = className;
    13	    }
    14	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    15	      node.textContent = text;
    16	    }

    20	  function appendChildren(parent, children) {
    21	    if (children === null || children === undefined) {
    22	      return;
    23	    }
    24	    if (Array.isArray(children)) {
    25	      children.forEach(function (child) {
    26	        if (child) {
    27	          parent.appendChild(child);
    28	        }
    29	      });
    30	    } else {
    31	      parent.appendChild(children);
    32	    }
    33	  }

    55	      anchor.appendChild(document.createTextNode(pair[1]));
    56	      if (name === "review") {
    57	        anchor.appendChild(badge);
    58	      }
    59	      linkEls[name] = anchor;
    60	      links.appendChild(anchor);
    61	    });
    62	    nav.appendChild(make("span", "shell-brand", "planner"));
    63	    nav.appendChild(links);
    64	    var content = make("main", "shell-content");
    65	    shell.appendChild(nav);
    66	    shell.appendChild(content);
    67	
    68	    function setReviewBadge(n) {
    69	      if (n > 0) {
    70	        badge.textContent = String(n);
    71	        badge.classList.remove("hidden");
    72	      } else {
    73	        badge.classList.add("hidden");
    74	      }
    75	    }
    76	    function setActiveNav(name) {
    77	      items.forEach(function (pair) {
    78	        linkEls[pair[0]].classList.toggle("active", pair[0] === name);
    79	      });
    80	    }
    81	    // The shell IS the returned DOM node (ticket: components return DOM nodes);
    82	    // the router's handles ride on it as properties.
    83	    shell.content = content;
    84	    shell.setReviewBadge = setReviewBadge;
    85	    shell.setActiveNav = setActiveNav;
    86	    return shell;
    87	  }

   100	  function markdownBlock(text) {
   101	    if (!text || !String(text).trim()) {
   102	      return make("div", "quiet-line", "(none)");
   103	    }

   109	  // 5.4 Field editor — no optimistic UI; a successful save is reflected by the
   110	  // WS-invalidation refetch.
   111	  function fieldEditor(value, onSave) {
   112	    var wrap = make("div", "field-editor");
   113	    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input");
   114	    textarea.rows = 8;
   115	    textarea.value = value || "";
   116	    var actions = make("div", "field-editor-actions");
   117	    var button = make("button", "button button--primary", "Save");
   118	    button.type = "button";
   119	    button.addEventListener("click", function () {
   120	      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
   121	      if (prior) {
   122	        actions.removeChild(prior);
   123	      }
   124	      button.disabled = true;
   125	      Promise.resolve(onSave(textarea.value)).then(
   126	        function () {},
   127	        function (err) {
   128	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   129	        }
   130	      ).then(function () {
   131	        button.disabled = false;
   132	      });
   133	    });
   134	    actions.appendChild(button);
   135	    wrap.appendChild(textarea);
   136	    wrap.appendChild(actions);
   137	    return wrap;
   138	  }

   150	  function chip(variant, value, opts) {
   151	    opts = opts || {};
   152	    var span = make("span", "chip");
   153	    if (variant === "priority") {
   154	      span.classList.add("chip--priority");
   155	      span.classList.add("chip--" + String(value).toLowerCase());
   156	      span.textContent = String(value);
   157	    } else if (variant === "state") {
   158	      span.classList.add("chip--state");
   159	      span.setAttribute("data-value", String(value));
   160	      span.textContent = String(value).replace(/_/g, " ");
   161	    } else if (variant === "project") {
   162	      span.classList.add("chip--project");
   163	      span.textContent = String(value);
   164	    } else if (variant === "deadline") {
   165	      span.classList.add("chip--deadline");
   166	      if (opts.overdue) {
   167	        span.classList.add("chip--overdue");
   168	      }
   169	      span.textContent = String(value);
   170	    } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(CHIP_MARKERS, variant)) {
   171	      span.classList.add("chip--" + variant);
   172	      span.textContent = CHIP_MARKERS[variant];
   173	    } else {
   174	      span.textContent = value === undefined || value === null ? "" : String(value);
   175	    }
   176	    return span;
   177	  }

   195	    line.appendChild(make("span", "error-message", (err && err.message) || "request failed"));
   196	    return line;
   197	  }

     1	/* Data layer: JSON fetch wrapper (structured-error passthrough), the WS client,
     2	 * and the invalidation bus (SPEC §9). No client-side state store — the bus holds
     3	 * only a cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff delay, and one flush callback; every
     4	 * flush makes the active screen refetch. WS batches are an invalidation signal:
     5	 * onmessage reads only msg.cursor, never the events array.
     6	 * Classic script: attaches Planner.api and Planner.bus; defines window.__plannerDebug. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	
    12	  // The sanctioned console hook: counters only, one console.debug per flush / open.
    13	  window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };
    14	
    15	  function makeError(code, message, extra) {
    16	    var err = new Error(message);
    17	    err.code = code;
    18	    if (extra) {
    19	      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
    20	        err[key] = extra[key];
    21	      });
    22	    }
    23	    return err;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  function makePlannerError(envelope, status) {
    27	    var err = new Error(envelope.message);
    28	    err.code = envelope.code;
    29	    err.detail = envelope.detail;
    30	    err.status = status;
    31	    err.isPlannerError = true;
    32	    return err;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  // --- fetchJson ---------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	  function fetchJson(path, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    var init = { method: options.method || "GET", headers: {} };
    40	    if (options.body !== undefined && options.body !== null) {
    41	      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    42	      init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    43	    }
    44	    return fetch(path, init).then(
    45	      function (response) {
    46	        return response.text().then(function (raw) {
    47	          return { response: response, raw: raw };
    48	        });
    49	      },
    50	      function () {
    51	        return Promise.reject(makeError("network", "network error"));
    52	      }
    53	    ).then(function (pair) {
    54	      var response = pair.response;
    55	      var raw = pair.raw;
    56	      if (response.ok) {
    57	        try {
    58	          return raw === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(raw);
    59	        } catch (parseErr) {
    60	          return Promise.reject(
    61	            makeError("bad_json", "invalid JSON in response", { status: response.status })
    62	          );
    63	        }
    64	      }
    65	      var parsed = null;
    66	      try {
    67	        parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    68	      } catch (parseErr) {
    69	        parsed = null;
    70	      }
    71	      if (parsed && parsed.error && parsed.error.code) {
    72	        return Promise.reject(makePlannerError(parsed.error, response.status));
    73	      }
    74	      return Promise.reject(
    75	        makeError("http_error", "HTTP " + response.status, { status: response.status })
    76	      );
    77	    });
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  Planner.api = { fetchJson: fetchJson };
    81	
    82	  // --- the invalidation bus ----------------------------------------------------
    83	
    84	  var cursor = 0;
    85	  var socket = null;
    86	  var debounceMs = config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    87	  var flushTimer = null;
    88	  var retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
    89	  var onFlush = null;
    90	  var started = false;
    91	
    92	  function flush() {
    93	    flushTimer = null;
    94	    window.__plannerDebug.flushes += 1;
    95	    console.debug("[planner] flush " + window.__plannerDebug.flushes);
    96	    if (onFlush) {
    97	      onFlush();
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function schedule() {
   102	    clearTimeout(flushTimer);
   103	    flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  function connect() {
   107	    var scheme = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
   108	    var url = scheme + location.host + config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor;
   109	    socket = new WebSocket(url);
   110	    socket.onopen = function () {
   111	      retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
   112	      window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1;
   113	      console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor);
   114	    };
   115	    socket.onmessage = function (event) {
   116	      var msg;
   117	      try {
   118	        msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
   119	      } catch (parseErr) {
   120	        console.debug("[planner] ws bad json");
   121	        return;
   122	      }
   123	      if (typeof msg.cursor === "number") {
   124	        cursor = msg.cursor;
   125	        window.__plannerDebug.cursor = cursor;
   126	      }
   127	      schedule();
   128	    };
   129	    socket.onclose = function () {
   130	      socket = null;
   131	      setTimeout(connect, retryMs);
   132	      retryMs = Math.min(retryMs * config.WS_RETRY_FACTOR, config.WS_RETRY_MAX_MS);
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  Planner.bus = {
   137	    onInvalidate: function (fn) {
   138	      onFlush = fn;
   139	    },
   140	    start: function (ms) {
   141	      if (started) {
   142	        return;
   143	      }
   144	      started = true;
   145	      debounceMs = ms;
   146	      connect();
   147	    }
   148	  };
   149	})();

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Link create/remove over the `links` table plus the blocked-derivation (§3.6,
     2	SPEC lines 54–57). Core infrastructure over the DB (like events.py) — sqlite3 lives
     3	here, not in dispatch/logic. Config-free: `now` is always a parameter."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	import sqlite3
     8	from collections import deque
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, LinkKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.core.events import append_event
    14	
    15	# blocks/parent_child are two independently-checked transitive relations (§3.6).
    16	_CYCLE_CHECKED: Final[frozenset[LinkKind]] = frozenset(
    17	    {LinkKind.blocks, LinkKind.parent_child}
    18	)
    19	# Endpoint prefix rules (D8): (allowed from-prefixes, allowed to-prefixes); None = any.
    20	_ENDPOINT_RULES: Final[dict[LinkKind, tuple[frozenset[str] | None, frozenset[str] | None]]] = {
    21	    LinkKind.belongs_to: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"si"})),
    22	    LinkKind.parent_child: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t"})),
    23	    LinkKind.blocks: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t", "si"})),
    24	    LinkKind.relates: (None, None),
    25	}
    26	
    27	
    28	def _prefix(entity_id: str) -> str:
    29	    """The endpoint kind is the id prefix before the first '_' (t_x -> 't')."""
    30	    return entity_id.split("_", 1)[0]
    31	
    32	
    33	def add_link(
    34	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
    35	) -> None:
    36	    """Create a link, enforcing §3.6: no self-links, fixed endpoint kinds per link,
    37	    at most one belongs_to per ticket, and no blocks/parent_child transitive cycle."""
    38	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
    39	    # 1. Self-link (§3.6): the two endpoints must differ. Pure check, outside the txn.
    40	    if from_id == to_id:
    41	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.link_invalid, "self-links are not allowed", detail)
    42	    # 2. Endpoint kinds (D8): the prefix fixes each side. Pure check, outside the txn.
    43	    from_rule, to_rule = _ENDPOINT_RULES[kind]
    44	    if from_rule is not None and _prefix(from_id) not in from_rule:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    47	            f"{kind.value} from-endpoint must be one of {sorted(from_rule)}",
    48	            detail,
    49	        )
    50	    if to_rule is not None and _prefix(to_id) not in to_rule:
    51	        raise PlannerError(
    52	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    53	            f"{kind.value} to-endpoint must be one of {sorted(to_rule)}",
    54	            detail,
    55	        )
    56	    # Steps 3–6 serialize under the write lock (A2): BEGIN IMMEDIATE takes the lock
    57	    # before the reads, so two connections cannot interleave BFS-then-insert to admit
    58	    # a cycle or a second belongs_to. Contention resolves via db.connect's busy_timeout.
    59	    own_txn = not conn.in_transaction
    60	    if own_txn:
    61	        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    62	    try:
    63	        # 3. belongs_to uniqueness (§3.6): at most one per ticket.
    64	        if kind is LinkKind.belongs_to:
    65	            existing = conn.execute(
    66	                "SELECT 1 FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind='belongs_to' LIMIT 1",
    67	                (from_id,),
    68	            ).fetchone()
    69	            if existing is not None:
    70	                raise PlannerError(
    71	                    ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    72	                    "ticket already has a belongs_to link",
    73	                    {"from_id": from_id},
    74	                )
    75	        # 4. Transitive cycle check (§3.6, D10): from->to makes a cycle iff `from` is
    76	        # reachable from `to` along same-kind edges. BFS from to_id, visited-guarded.
    77	        if kind in _CYCLE_CHECKED:
    78	            queue: deque[str] = deque([to_id])
    79	            visited: set[str] = {to_id}
    80	            while queue:
    81	                node = queue.popleft()
    82	                if node == from_id:
    83	                    raise PlannerError(
    84	                        ErrorCode.link_cycle,
    85	                        f"link would create a {kind.value} cycle",
    86	                        detail,
    87	                    )
    88	                rows = conn.execute(
    89	                    "SELECT to_id FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind=?",
    90	                    (node, kind.value),
    91	                ).fetchall()
    92	                for succ in rows:
    93	                    nxt = str(succ["to_id"])
    94	                    if nxt not in visited:
    95	                        visited.add(nxt)
    96	                        queue.append(nxt)
    97	        # 5. Insert; any IntegrityError (PK dup, partial unique index race, CHECK) is
    98	        # re-raised as link_invalid (D9) — a raw IntegrityError never escapes.
    99	        try:
   100	            conn.execute(
   101	                "INSERT INTO links (from_id, to_id, kind) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   102	                (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   103	            )
   104	        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
   105	            raise PlannerError(
   106	                ErrorCode.link_invalid,
   107	                "link already exists or violates a link constraint",
   108	                detail,
   109	            ) from exc
   110	        # 6. Event.
   111	        append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_added, detail, now)
   112	    except BaseException:
   113	        if own_txn:
   114	            conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
   115	        raise
   116	    if own_txn:
   117	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
   118	
   119	
   120	def remove_link(
   121	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
   122	) -> None:
   123	    """Delete a link; a missing link is not_found (D12)."""
   124	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
   125	    cursor = conn.execute(
   126	        "DELETE FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND to_id=? AND kind=?",
   127	        (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   128	    )
   129	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   130	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "link not found", detail)
   131	    append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_removed, detail, now)
   132	
   133	
   134	def blocked_target_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> set[str]:
   135	    """Ids (tickets or sprint items) currently blocked: targets of a blocks link
   136	    whose source ticket is not done/dropped (§3.6 line 57)."""
   137	    rows = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT DISTINCT l.to_id FROM links l "
   139	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   140	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped')"
   141	    ).fetchall()
   142	    return {str(row["to_id"]) for row in rows}
   143	
   144	
   145	def is_blocked(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str) -> bool:
   146	    """Whether one entity is blocked. The join to tickets makes the rule literal:
   147	    only a ticket source in an open state blocks; a missing/non-ticket source never
   148	    does (D8)."""
   149	    row = conn.execute(
   150	        "SELECT 1 FROM links l "
   151	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   152	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped') "
   153	        "AND l.to_id = ? LIMIT 1",
   154	        (entity_id,),
   155	    ).fetchone()
   156	    return row is not None

    71	def create_app(
    72	    config: Config,
    73	    clock: Clock,
    74	    adapters: Adapters,
    75	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    76	) -> FastAPI:
    77	    # The DDL carries the literal 200 title cap; the config value is what write
    78	    # paths enforce. Guard that they agree (amendment 11). Factory time is startup
    79	    # and strictly earlier than any request, so this fires even for TestClient apps
    80	    # that never enter the lifespan.

    85	        Path(config.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    93	                _log.warning("planner.core.loops unavailable; running without background loops")
    94	            else:
    95	                loops = start(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
    96	        try:
    97	            yield
    98	        finally:
    99	            if loops is not None:
   100	                await loops.stop()

     1	"""Ticket domain shapes: the state machine order, the four fields, the grant pair,
     2	and the ticket row. Stdlib only; Priority/Project are imported from core (shared
     3	vocabulary), never redeclared."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8	from enum import StrEnum
     9	from typing import Final, Literal
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import Priority, Project
    12	
    13	
    14	class TicketState(StrEnum):        # §4.1, exact order
    15	    needs_success = "needs_success"
    16	    needs_approach = "needs_approach"
    17	    needs_plan = "needs_plan"
    18	    in_progress = "in_progress"
    19	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    20	    done = "done"
    21	    dropped = "dropped"            # terminal, human-only, outside the linear order
    22	
    23	
    24	# Linear pipeline order (dropped excluded). Index comparison implements "<= ceiling".
    25	# ceiling and next_ceiling values are restricted to members of this tuple (never dropped).
    26	STATE_ORDER: Final[tuple[TicketState, ...]] = (
    27	    TicketState.needs_success, TicketState.needs_approach, TicketState.needs_plan,
    28	    TicketState.in_progress, TicketState.needs_review, TicketState.done,
    29	)
    30	
    31	
    32	class FieldName(StrEnum):          # §4.2 — the exactly-four field keys
    33	    success = "success"
    34	    approach = "approach"
    35	    plan = "plan"
    36	    result = "result"
    37	
    38	
    39	class AtCap(StrEnum):              # §4.3
    40	    stop = "stop"
    41	    propose = "propose"
    42	
    43	
    44	# §4.2 table — gating field per pre-terminal state. needs_review has none (human approve).
    45	GATING_FIELD: Final[dict[TicketState, FieldName]] = {
    46	    TicketState.needs_success: FieldName.success,
    47	    TicketState.needs_approach: FieldName.approach,
    48	    TicketState.needs_plan: FieldName.plan,
    49	    TicketState.in_progress: FieldName.result,
    50	}
    51	
    52	# §4.2 table — accepted proposal advances to. in_progress -> needs_review is the
    53	# default route; the ceiling=done special case (§4.4.5) is resolution-engine logic,
    54	# not a second table entry.
    55	ADVANCE_TARGET: Final[dict[TicketState, TicketState]] = {
    56	    TicketState.needs_success: TicketState.needs_approach,
    57	    TicketState.needs_approach: TicketState.needs_plan,
    58	    TicketState.needs_plan: TicketState.in_progress,
    59	    TicketState.in_progress: TicketState.needs_review,
    60	}
    61	
    62	
    63	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    64	class Proposal:                    # §4.2 proposal slot
    65	    body: str
    66	    proposed_by: str               # actor string: "agent", run id context, or PLAN_ACTOR
    67	    created_at: int
    68	
    69	
    70	@dataclass
    71	class FieldSlot:                   # §4.2 — one of the four field objects
    72	    value: str | None = None       # canonical; resolution engine is the only writer
    73	    proposal: Proposal | None = None
    74	    notes: str | None = None       # free guidance; result.notes = review notes (§4.2)
    75	
    76	
    77	@dataclass
    78	class TicketFields:                # tickets.fields JSON column, exactly four keys
    79	    success: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    80	    approach: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    81	    plan: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    82	    result: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    83	
    84	
    85	# --- the grant pair (§4.4.7) ---
    86	NO_FURTHER: Final = "none"                     # wire sentinel: ceiling = the newly entered state
    87	NextCeiling = TicketState | Literal["none"]    # valid TicketState values are STATE_ORDER members
    88	
    89	
    90	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    91	class GrantPair:                   # required on every human accept/edit-accept
    92	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling
    93	    at_cap: AtCap
    94	
    95	
    96	@dataclass
    97	class Ticket:                      # §3.3 — column names match exactly
    98	    id: str
    99	    title: str                     # <= title_max_chars (200), every write path
   100	    state: TicketState
   101	    priority: Priority             # default P3
   102	    deadline: str | None           # ISO date
   103	    project: Project | None        # NULL when parented (derived)
   104	    sprint_item_id: str | None
   105	    sprint_id: str | None          # writable only when sprint_item_id IS NULL
   106	    recap: str                     # writable only past needs_success
   107	    ceiling: TicketState           # default needs_success (R2); restricted to STATE_ORDER
   108	    at_cap: AtCap                  # default propose (R2)
   109	    auto_blocked: bool
   110	    consecutive_failures: int      # §7.5
   111	    chat_session_key: str | None
   112	    alias: str | None              # migration "Ticket ID:" (§12), unique when present
   113	    fields: TicketFields
   114	    claim_lock: str | None         # §7.3 claim token; NULL = unclaimed
   115	    claim_expires: int | None
   116	    created_at: int
   117	    updated_at: int

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""State-machine math for §4.2/§4.3/§4.4: linear order indexing, gating and
     2	advance tables, the auto-accept condition, ceiling comparison, and onward-grant
     3	resolution. Pure: contracts/errors + fields_codec only."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
     8	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
     9	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    10	    GATING_FIELD,
    11	    NO_FURTHER,
    12	    STATE_ORDER,
    13	    AtCap,
    14	    FieldName,
    15	    GrantPair,
    16	    NextCeiling,
    17	    TicketFields,
    18	    TicketState,
    19	)
    20	from planner.tickets.logic import fields_codec
    21	
    22	
    23	def state_index(state: TicketState) -> int:
    24	    try:
    25	        return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
    26	    except ValueError as exc:
    27	        raise PlannerError(
    28	            ErrorCode.validation, "state outside the linear order", {"state": state.value}
    29	        ) from exc
    30	
    31	
    32	def is_terminal(state: TicketState) -> bool:
    33	    return state in (TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped)
    34	
    35	
    36	def gating_field(state: TicketState) -> FieldName | None:
    37	    return GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    38	
    39	
    40	def advance_target(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> TicketState:
    41	    if state is TicketState.in_progress and ceiling is TicketState.done:
    42	        return TicketState.done
    43	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET.get(state)
    44	    if target is None:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.validation, "state has no advance target", {"state": state.value}
    47	        )
    48	    return target
    49	
    50	
    51	def auto_accept_target(
    52	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, field: FieldName
    53	) -> TicketState | None:
    54	    if is_terminal(state):
    55	        return None
    56	    if field is not gating_field(state):
    57	        return None
    58	    target = advance_target(state, ceiling)
    59	    if state_index(target) > state_index(ceiling):
    60	        return None
    61	    return target
    62	
    63	
    64	def at_or_beyond_ceiling(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> bool:
    65	    return state_index(state) >= state_index(ceiling)
    66	
    67	
    68	def validate_ceiling(ceiling: TicketState) -> None:
    69	    if ceiling not in STATE_ORDER:
    70	        raise PlannerError(
    71	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "ceiling must be a linear state", {"ceiling": ceiling.value}
    72	        )
    73	
    74	
    75	def resolve_grant(
    76	    new_state: TicketState, next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None, at_cap: AtCap | None
    77	) -> GrantPair:
    78	    if next_ceiling is None or at_cap is None:
    79	        missing: list[str] = []
    80	        if next_ceiling is None:
    81	            missing.append("next_ceiling")
    82	        if at_cap is None:
    83	            missing.append("at_cap")
    84	        raise PlannerError(
    85	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "accept requires the onward grant pair", {"missing": missing}
    86	        )
    87	    if next_ceiling == NO_FURTHER:
    88	        # the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state
    89	        return GrantPair(next_ceiling=new_state, at_cap=at_cap)
    90	    if not isinstance(next_ceiling, TicketState):
    91	        raise PlannerError(
    92	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": str(next_ceiling)}
    93	        )
    94	    if next_ceiling not in STATE_ORDER or state_index(next_ceiling) < state_index(new_state):
    95	        raise PlannerError(
    96	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid,
    97	            "next_ceiling must be at or beyond the new state",
    98	            {"next_ceiling": next_ceiling.value, "new_state": new_state.value},
    99	        )
   100	    return GrantPair(next_ceiling=next_ceiling, at_cap=at_cap)
   101	
   102	
   103	def has_pending_gating_proposal(state: TicketState, fields: TicketFields) -> bool:
   104	    field = gating_field(state)
   105	    if field is None:
   106	        return False
   107	    return fields_codec.get_slot(fields, field).proposal is not None

orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:19:**Coding style (binding, from T14):** classic IIFE `(function(){ "use strict"; var Planner = window.Planner … })()`; DOM built with `document.createElement` / `textContent` / `setAttribute` only — **no `innerHTML`** (the only `innerHTML` in the repo is `markdown.js`). Screens do not import `make`/`appendChildren` (those are private to components.js); they call `document.createElement` directly and compose `Planner.components.*`. No client-side state store, no `navigate()` — anchors carry state in the URL.
orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:21:**Rendering model (binding, from T14):** `render(root, params)` rebuilds the whole screen from fresh fetches. After a **successful** mutating action the code does **not** re-render — the WS invalidation bus debounce-flushes, `app.js onFlush()` re-runs `route()`, and `route()` calls the screen's `render` again on a fresh `screenDiv`. Buttons disable while a request is in flight and re-enable after (mirror `fieldEditor` in components.js lines 111–138). A failed action renders `Planner.components.errorLine(err)` inline near the control that failed; it does **not** replace the screen.

    13	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError

    21	def _demo_report(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> MigrationReport:
    22	    def count(sql: str) -> int:
    23	        return int(conn.execute(sql).fetchone()[0])
    24	
    25	    return MigrationReport(
    26	        sprints=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprints"),
    27	        sprint_items=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprint_items WHERE sprint_id IS NOT NULL"),
    28	        deferred_items=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sprint_items WHERE sprint_id IS NULL"),
    29	        tickets=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tickets"),
    30	        ideas=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ideas"),
    31	        links=count("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM links WHERE kind = 'belongs_to'"),
    32	        duplicates_skipped=0,
    33	        skipped=[],
    34	    )
    35	
    36	
    37	@router.post("/seed")
    38	async def seed(body: dict[str, Any], request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    39	    source_dir = body.get("source_dir")
    40	    demo = bool(body.get("demo", False))
    41	    has_source = source_dir is not None
    42	    if has_source == demo:  # both present, or neither
    43	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "provide exactly one of source_dir or demo")
    44	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection] = request.app.state.conn_factory
    45	    conn = conn_factory()
    46	    try:
    47	        if demo:
    48	            seed_demo(conn)  # raises db_not_empty on a non-empty database
    49	            report = _demo_report(conn)
    50	        else:
    51	            if not isinstance(source_dir, str) or not Path(source_dir).is_dir():
    52	                raise PlannerError(
    53	                    ErrorCode.validation, "source_dir must be an existing directory"
    54	                )
    55	            report = seed_from_source(conn, source_dir)
    56	    finally:
    57	        conn.close()
    58	    return asdict(report)

    91	def item_rollup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    92	    rollup: dict[str, int] = {s.value: 0 for s in TicketState}
    93	    rows = conn.execute(

    95	        (item_id,),

   106	def list_items(
   107	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   108	    *,
   109	    status: ItemStatus | None,
   110	    project: Project | None,
   111	    sprint_id_filter: str | None,
   112	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   113	    clauses: list[str] = []
   114	    params: list[str] = []
   115	    if status is not None:
   116	        clauses.append("status = ?")

   122	        if sprint_id_filter == "null":
   123	            clauses.append("sprint_id IS NULL")
   124	        else:
   125	            clauses.append("sprint_id = ?")
   126	            params.append(sprint_id_filter)
   127	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   128	    rows = conn.execute(
   129	        "SELECT id, priority, created_at FROM sprint_items" + where, tuple(params)
   130	    ).fetchall()

   134	        result.append({**item_json(read.item), "blockers_cleared": read.blockers_cleared})
   135	    return result

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Claim/TTL lease arithmetic — the single definition of lease time (§7.3). Pure
     2	stdlib, no imports. Every dispatch path (candidate assembly, heartbeat guard, close
     3	guard, reclaim sweep) computes expiry and 'is this lease still alive?' through these
     4	three functions, so the boundary rule lives in exactly one place."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	
     9	def expiry_at(now: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int:
    10	    """Expiry for a claim taken or heartbeaten at `now`: exactly now + one TTL."""
    11	    return now + ttl_seconds
    12	
    13	
    14	def is_expired(claim_expires: int | None, now: int) -> bool:
    15	    """Expired iff claim_expires <= now — the lease is the half-open interval
    16	    [start, start + ttl); a NULL expiry counts as expired. Half-open means a
    17	    heartbeat/close at exactly claim_expires fails while a reclaim at exactly
    18	    claim_expires proceeds, so no instant lets a writer and the reclaimer both
    19	    think the lease is live."""
    20	    return claim_expires is None or claim_expires <= now
    21	
    22	
    23	def has_active_claim(claim_lock: str | None, claim_expires: int | None, now: int) -> bool:
    24	    """§7.2 'no active claim' / contract 'claim_lock set and unexpired'."""
    25	    return claim_lock is not None and not is_expired(claim_expires, now)

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/tickets/data.py | sed -n '1,360p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The only module that writes ticket rows. State, ceiling/at_cap, and fields
     2	value mutations happen in exactly one function (_apply_decision); every public
     3	writer is one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction. Plain field writers (note, recap,
     4	priority, deadline, sprint) do their own single-column UPDATE and never touch
     5	state/ceiling/at_cap/fields.value. sqlite3, events and ids live here only; the
     6	clock arrives as now (unix seconds) and the title limit as an argument."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from collections.abc import Iterator
    12	from contextlib import contextmanager
    13	
    14	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, Priority, Project
    15	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    16	from planner.core.events import append_event
    17	from planner.core.ids import ID_PREFIXES, new_id
    18	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    19	    AtCap,
    20	    FieldName,
    21	    FieldSlot,
    22	    NextCeiling,
    23	    Ticket,
    24	    TicketFields,
    25	    TicketState,
    26	)
    27	from planner.tickets.logic import admission, fields_codec, resolution
    28	from planner.tickets.logic.decisions import Decision
    29	
    30	
    31	@contextmanager
    32	def _txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    33	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    34	    try:
    35	        yield
    36	    except BaseException:
    37	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    38	        raise
    39	    else:
    40	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    41	
    42	
    43	def _row_to_ticket(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Ticket:
    44	    project_raw = row["project"]
    45	    return Ticket(
    46	        id=row["id"],
    47	        title=row["title"],
    48	        state=TicketState(row["state"]),
    49	        priority=Priority(row["priority"]),
    50	        deadline=row["deadline"],
    51	        project=Project(project_raw) if project_raw is not None else None,
    52	        sprint_item_id=row["sprint_item_id"],
    53	        sprint_id=row["sprint_id"],
    54	        recap=row["recap"],
    55	        ceiling=TicketState(row["ceiling"]),
    56	        at_cap=AtCap(row["at_cap"]),
    57	        auto_blocked=bool(row["auto_blocked"]),
    58	        consecutive_failures=row["consecutive_failures"],
    59	        chat_session_key=row["chat_session_key"],
    60	        alias=row["alias"],
    61	        fields=fields_codec.fields_from_json(row["fields"]),
    62	        claim_lock=row["claim_lock"],
    63	        claim_expires=row["claim_expires"],
    64	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    65	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    66	    )
    67	
    68	
    69	def _load_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> Ticket:
    70	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM tickets WHERE id = ?", (ticket_id,)).fetchone()
    71	    if row is None:
    72	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
    73	    return _row_to_ticket(row)
    74	
    75	
    76	def _apply_decision(
    77	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket: Ticket, decision: Decision, now: int
    78	) -> Ticket:
    79	    new_fields = decision.new_fields if decision.new_fields is not None else ticket.fields
    80	    new_state = decision.new_state if decision.new_state is not None else ticket.state
    81	    new_ceiling = decision.new_ceiling if decision.new_ceiling is not None else ticket.ceiling
    82	    new_at_cap = decision.new_at_cap if decision.new_at_cap is not None else ticket.at_cap
    83	    conn.execute(
    84	        "UPDATE tickets SET fields = ?, state = ?, ceiling = ?, at_cap = ?, updated_at = ? "
    85	        "WHERE id = ?",
    86	        (
    87	            fields_codec.fields_to_json(new_fields),
    88	            new_state.value,
    89	            new_ceiling.value,
    90	            new_at_cap.value,
    91	            now,
    92	            ticket.id,
    93	        ),
    94	    )
    95	    for spec in decision.events:
    96	        append_event(conn, ticket.id, spec.kind, spec.payload, now)
    97	    return _load_ticket(conn, ticket.id)
    98	
    99	
   100	def create_ticket(
   101	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   102	    *,
   103	    title: str,
   104	    actor: str,
   105	    now: int,
   106	    title_max_chars: int,
   107	    project: Project | None = None,
   108	    priority: Priority = Priority.P3,
   109	    deadline: str | None = None,
   110	    sprint_id: str | None = None,
   111	    sprint_item_id: str | None = None,
   112	) -> Ticket:
   113	    admission.validate_title(title, title_max_chars)
   114	    admission.validate_deadline(deadline)
   115	    ticket_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["ticket"])
   116	    empty_fields = fields_codec.fields_to_json(TicketFields())
   117	    with _txn(conn):
   118	        if sprint_item_id is not None:
   119	            if conn.execute(
   120	                "SELECT 1 FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (sprint_item_id,)
   121	            ).fetchone() is None:
   122	                raise PlannerError(
   123	                    ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint item not found", {"sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id}
   124	                )
   125	            if sprint_id is not None:
   126	                raise PlannerError(
   127	                    ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
   128	                    "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
   129	                    {"sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
   130	                )
   131	            if project is not None:
   132	                raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is derived when parented")
   133	        elif sprint_id is not None:
   134	            exists = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM sprints WHERE id = ?", (sprint_id,)).fetchone()
   135	            if exists is None:
   136	                raise PlannerError(
   137	                    ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint not found", {"sprint_id": sprint_id}
   138	                )
   139	        conn.execute(
   140	            "INSERT INTO tickets (id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, "
   141	            "sprint_id, recap, ceiling, at_cap, auto_blocked, consecutive_failures, "
   142	            "chat_session_key, alias, fields, claim_lock, claim_expires, created_at, updated_at) "
   143	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '', ?, ?, 0, 0, NULL, NULL, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)",
   144	            (
   145	                ticket_id,
   146	                title,
   147	                TicketState.needs_success.value,
   148	                priority.value,
   149	                deadline,
   150	                project.value if project is not None else None,
   151	                sprint_item_id,
   152	                sprint_id,
   153	                TicketState.needs_success.value,
   154	                AtCap.propose.value,
   155	                empty_fields,
   156	                now,
   157	                now,
   158	            ),
   159	        )
   160	        append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.ticket_created, {}, now)
   161	        return _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   162	
   163	
   164	def read_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> Ticket:
   165	    return _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   166	
   167	
   168	def get_effective_sprint_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str | None:
   169	    ticket = _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   170	    if ticket.sprint_item_id is None:
   171	        return ticket.sprint_id
   172	    row = conn.execute(
   173	        "SELECT sprint_id FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (ticket.sprint_item_id,)
   174	    ).fetchone()
   175	    if row is None:
   176	        return None
   177	    sprint_id: str | None = row["sprint_id"]
   178	    return sprint_id
   179

    14	HUMAN_ACTOR: Final[str] = "human"
    15	
    16	
    17	def is_human(actor: str) -> bool:
    18	    return actor == HUMAN_ACTOR
    19	
    20	
    21	def require_human(actor: str, action: str) -> None:
    22	    if actor != HUMAN_ACTOR:
    23	        raise PlannerError(
    24	            ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
    25	            f"{action} is a human-only action",
    26	            {"action": action, "actor": actor},
    27	        )
    28	
    29	
    30	def check_agent_proposal(
    31	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, field: FieldName
    32	) -> None:
    33	    if machine.is_terminal(state):
    34	        raise PlannerError(
    35	            ErrorCode.validation, "no proposals on a terminal ticket", {"state": state.value}
    36	        )
    37	    gating = machine.gating_field(state)
    38	    if not machine.at_or_beyond_ceiling(state, ceiling):
    39	        return
    40	    if at_cap is AtCap.stop:
    41	        raise PlannerError(
    42	            ErrorCode.at_cap_stop,
    43	            "ticket is at its ceiling with at_cap=stop",
    44	            {
    45	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    46	                "state": state.value,
    47	                "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    48	                "at_cap": "stop",
    49	            },
    50	        )
    51	    if field is not gating:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation,
    54	            "at the ceiling agents may propose only the current gating field",
    55	            {
    56	                "field": field.value,
    57	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    58	                "state": state.value,
    59	            },
    60	        )
    61	
    62	
    63	def check_recap_writable(state: TicketState) -> None:
    64	    if state is TicketState.needs_success or state is TicketState.dropped:
    65	        raise PlannerError(
    66	            ErrorCode.recap_too_early,
    67	            "recap is writable only past needs_success",
    68	            {"state": state.value},
    69	        )
    70	
    71	
    72	def check_sprint_assignable(ticket_id: str, sprint_item_id: str | None) -> None:
    73	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
    74	        raise PlannerError(
    75	            ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
    76	            "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
    77	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
    78	        )
    79	
    80	
    81	def validate_title(title: str, max_chars: int) -> None:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "title must be non-empty")
    84	    if len(title) > max_chars:
    85	        raise PlannerError(
    86	            ErrorCode.title_too_long,
    87	            f"title exceeds {max_chars} characters",
    88	            {"length": len(title), "max": max_chars},
    89	        )
    90	
    91	
    92	def validate_deadline(deadline: str | None) -> None:
    93	    if deadline is None:
    94	        return
    95	    try:
    96	        date.fromisoformat(deadline)
    97	    except ValueError as exc:
    98	        raise PlannerError(
    99	            ErrorCode.validation, "deadline must be an ISO date", {"deadline": deadline}
   100	        ) from exc
   101	
   102	
   103	def validate_body(body: str, what: str) -> None:
   104	    if not body:
   105	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"{what} must be non-empty")

    57	def _parse_bool(raw: object, key: str) -> bool:
    58	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    59	        return raw
    60	    text = str(raw).strip().lower()
    61	    if text in _TRUE:
    62	        return True
    63	    if text in _FALSE:
    64	        return False
    65	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid boolean for {key}: {raw!r}")
    66	
    67	
    68	def _parse_int(raw: object, key: str) -> int:
    69	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    70	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    71	    if isinstance(raw, int):
    72	        return raw
    73	    if isinstance(raw, str):
    74	        try:
    75	            return int(raw.strip())
    76	        except ValueError as exc:
    77	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}") from exc
    78	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")

    81	def _read_yaml(path: str | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    82	    resolved = Path(path) if path is not None else Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH)
    83	    if not resolved.exists():
    84	        return {}
    85	    loaded = yaml.safe_load(resolved.read_text())
    86	    if loaded is None:
    87	        return {}
    88	    if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
    89	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"config root must be a mapping: {resolved}")
    90	    return loaded

     1	"""The chat passthrough (§11). Resolves the chat entity (ticket or day), sends
     2	through the gateway adapter, and — on the first reply only — persists the minted
     3	session key onto the entity and logs one chat_session_created event in a single
     4	transaction. Framework-free: no FastAPI/pydantic (SPEC §14 / server.py docstring).
     5	Times arrive as unix-second ints from the caller's clock."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import logging
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from collections.abc import Iterator
    12	from contextlib import contextmanager
    13	from datetime import date
    14	
    15	from planner.chat.contracts import ChatSendResult, GatewayStatus
    16	from planner.core.adapters.base import GatewayAdapter
    17	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    18	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    19	from planner.core.events import append_event
    20	from planner.days.data import read_day
    21	
    22	_log = logging.getLogger("planner.chat")
    23	
    24	
    25	@contextmanager
    26	def _txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    27	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    28	    try:
    29	        yield
    30	    except BaseException:
    31	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    32	        raise
    33	    else:
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	
    36	
    37	def _resolve(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str, now: int) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    38	    if entity_id.startswith("t_"):
    39	        row = conn.execute(
    40	            "SELECT chat_session_key FROM tickets WHERE id = ?", (entity_id,)
    41	        ).fetchone()
    42	        if row is None:
    43	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"entity_id": entity_id})
    44	        key: str | None = row["chat_session_key"]
    45	        return "ticket", key
    46	    if entity_id.startswith("day_"):
    47	        # A day id is a canonical YYYY-MM-DD; round-trip the suffix because
    48	        # fromisoformat alone accepts compact variants. A non-canonical id names
    49	        # no day, so it is not_found rather than a materialized empty day.
    50	        raw = entity_id[len("day_") :]
    51	        try:
    52	            canonical = date.fromisoformat(raw).isoformat() == raw
    53	        except ValueError:
    54	            canonical = False
    55	        if not canonical:
    56	            raise PlannerError(
    57	                ErrorCode.not_found, "no chattable entity for id", {"entity_id": entity_id}
    58	            )
    59	        day = read_day(conn, entity_id, now)  # §3.4: materializes if absent
    60	        return "day", day.chat_session_key
    61	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "no chattable entity for id", {"entity_id": entity_id})
    62	
    63	
    64	def send(
    65	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    66	    gateway: GatewayAdapter,
    67	    entity_id: str,
    68	    text: str,
    69	    now: int,
    70	) -> ChatSendResult:
    71	    kind, stored_key = _resolve(conn, entity_id, now)
    72	    # The gateway call is IO; it runs outside any transaction.
    73	    try:
    74	        result = gateway.send(stored_key, entity_id, text)
    75	    except PlannerError:
    76	        raise  # already structured (offline adapter raises gateway_offline)
    77	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
    78	        raise PlannerError(
    79	            ErrorCode.gateway_offline, "gateway unavailable", {"cause": str(exc)}
    80	        ) from exc
    81	    if stored_key is None:  # first reply → persist the key and log one event
    82	        table = "tickets" if kind == "ticket" else "days"  # fixed map, never request input
    83	        with _txn(conn):
    84	            cursor = conn.execute(
    85	                f"UPDATE {table} SET chat_session_key = ?, updated_at = ? "
    86	                "WHERE id = ? AND chat_session_key IS NULL",
    87	                (result.session_key, now, entity_id),
    88	            )
    89	            if cursor.rowcount == 1:
    90	                append_event(
    91	                    conn,
    92	                    entity_id,
    93	                    EventKind.chat_session_created,
    94	                    {"session_key": result.session_key},
    95	                    now,
    96	                )
    97	            else:  # a concurrent first send won under the write lock; adopt its key
    98	                row = conn.execute(
    99	                    f"SELECT chat_session_key FROM {table} WHERE id = ?", (entity_id,)
   100	                ).fetchone()
   101	                result = ChatSendResult(
   102	                    reply_text=result.reply_text, session_key=row["chat_session_key"]
   103	                )
   104	    return result
   105	
   106	
   107	def status(gateway: GatewayAdapter) -> GatewayStatus:
   108	    result = gateway.status()
   109	    if result.detail is not None:
   110	        _log.info("gateway status detail: %s", result.detail)  # logged, not shown
   111	    return result

orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/plan.md:6:**Approach.** Five classic scripts compose under one namespace object `window.Planner` (each file is an IIFE that does `window.Planner = window.Planner || {}` and attaches exactly one sub-namespace), so seven asset files load in `<script src>` order with no bundler and every file passes `node --check`.
