orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/plan-review.md:43:**C4 (MAJOR) — two-adjacent-POSTs ⇒ one-flush assertion called timing-racy.** ACCEPTED AS DOCUMENTED RESIDUAL RISK, NOT CHANGED. For the assertion to fail, the second batch would have to arrive more than debounce (250ms) after the first — i.e. two sequential localhost POSTs separated by >200ms with the server polling at 50ms. Typical separation is <20ms. The plan pinned this exact risk and its only sanctioned remedy (widen the settle sleep; never weaken the exactly-one assertion — amendment A5). A fully deterministic variant would need server-side batching control that is outside this ticket's owned files. Two consecutive full smoke runs passed.

 succeeded in 0ms:
   120	- **Grant discipline** (when `requireGrant`): a local `sync()` sets `accept.disabled = inFlight || picker.getGrant() === null`. `sync` runs once at build (Accept starts disabled — nothing is picked) and is the picker's `onChange`. When `requireGrant` is false the button starts enabled (non-gating accepts carry no grant — resolution.py:174-187).
   121	- **Edited-body rule (exact):** on Accept, `payload.edited_body` is included **iff `textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body`** (strict string comparison against the body string from this render's fetch); when equal the key is omitted entirely, so the server records a plain accept (`edited: false`, resolution.py:151-161). Whitespace-only changes count as edits — deliberate: the server stores edited text exactly (resolution.py:161), and second-guessing the human's whitespace would be cleverness.
   122	- When `requireGrant`, `payload.next_ceiling` and `payload.at_cap` come from `picker.getGrant()` (never defaults — getGrant is null until both halves are picked, so Accept can't fire without them).
   123	- Click: remove any prior `.error-line` in the actions row; set `inFlight = true`, `sync()`; call `opts.onAccept(payload)`; on rejection prepend `errorLine(err)` to the actions row; finally `inFlight = false`, `sync()`. No optimistic UI — success is reflected by the WS flush re-render (the accept writes events → flush → refetch).

   125	### 1.5 `grantPairPicker(newState, onChange) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 8
   126	
   127	```js
   128	var _atcapSeq = 0;
   129	function grantPairPicker(newState, onChange) { ... }
   130	// newState: the state the accept newly enters (wire value, e.g. "needs_approach")
   131	// onChange: function() — called on every input change (host runs its enable/disable sync)
   132	// Returned element carries a .getGrant property:
   133	//   getGrant() -> {next_ceiling, at_cap} | null   (null until BOTH halves are explicit)
   134	```
   135	
   136	DOM sketch:
   137	
   138	```
   139	div.grant-picker [amber family — see §4]
   140	  label.grant-label  span "how far"
   141	    select.grant-ceiling [data-grant-ceiling]
   142	      option value="" disabled selected hidden  ""        <- placeholder; value "" = not picked
   143	      option value="none"          "No further"
   144	      option value=<s>             <s with "_"->" ">      <- one per STATE_ORDER state with index >= index(newState)
   145	  span.grant-atcap [data-grant-atcap]
   146	    label  input[type=radio name="atcap-"+(++_atcapSeq) value="stop"]     span "Stop"
   147	    label  input[type=radio name=<same>            value="propose"]       span "Propose"
   148	```
   149	
   150	- **Ceiling options:** `"No further"` (wire value `"none"`, the NO_FURTHER sentinel — contracts.py:86-87) plus every `STATE_ORDER` state at-or-beyond `newState` (`STATE_ORDER.slice(STATE_ORDER.indexOf(newState))`) — exactly the states `resolve_grant` accepts (machine.py:94-99, floor = the newly entered state). For `newState === "done"` (result accept on a ceiling=done ticket) the list is `No further` + `done` — the pair is still mandatory (§4.4.7 "even when the answer is nothing further").
   151	- **Neither half defaults to picked:** the select's placeholder option is `value=""` + `disabled selected hidden` (so the control shows empty and `""` can never be re-chosen); the radios share a per-instance unique name and neither is checked. `getGrant()` returns null unless `select.value !== ""` AND one radio is checked; otherwise `{next_ceiling: select.value, at_cap: checkedRadio.value}`.
   152	- Both the select's `change` and each radio's `change` call `onChange` — that plus `getGrant()` is the "host knows when BOTH halves are explicitly picked" surface.

   188	### 1.7 `chatPanel(entityId, opts) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 10
   189	
   190	```js
   191	function chatPanel(entityId, opts) { ... }
   192	// entityId: the SERVER-provided chat entity id (day.id or ticket.id) — never constructed client-side
   193	// opts = { available: bool }   // from GET /api/chat/<entityId>/status, fetched by the host screen
   194	```
   195	
   196	DOM sketch:
   197	
   198	```
   199	div.chat-panel [data-chat-panel]
   200	  div.chat-messages [data-chat-messages]
   201	    per {who, text} in chatTranscripts[entityId] (or []):
   202	      who "you"     -> div.chat-msg.chat-msg--you     [data-chat-msg="you"]      (plain textContent)
   203	      who "planner" -> div.chat-msg.chat-msg--planner [data-chat-msg="planner"]  (contains markdownBlock(text))
   204	  if !opts.available:
   205	      div.chat-offline [data-chat-offline] "gateway offline"        <- the §11 notice; no input source rendered
   206	  else:
   207	      (errorLine slot)
   208	      <input-source element> from Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx)
   209	```
   210	
   211	`ctx` wiring:
   212	
   213	- `ctx.initialText = chatDrafts[entityId] || ""`; `ctx.onInput = function (t) { chatDrafts[entityId] = t; }` — the draft survives the flush re-renders the Day screen constantly undergoes (every day action fires events).
   214	- `ctx.submit(text)`:
   215	  1. push `{who: "you", text: text}` onto `chatTranscripts[entityId]` (creating the array if absent); delete `chatDrafts[entityId]`; repaint the message list (rebuild `.chat-messages` children from the store — a private `paint()` closure).
   216	  2. `return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {method: "POST", body: {text: text}})` chained: on success push `{who: "planner", text: res.reply_text}` and repaint **iff the panel's root is still connected** (`root.isConnected`) — if a flush replaced the panel mid-flight, the store still has the reply and the next render shows it; on rejection prepend `errorLine(err)` above the input slot (keep the user's message in the store — it was said).
   217	- **Transcript survival mechanism, spelled out:** WS flush → `route()` → the screen re-renders → the screen calls `chatPanel(entityId, ...)` again → the new panel rebuilds its list from module-scope `chatTranscripts[entityId]`, which the flush did not touch. The first send's reply lands *before* its own `chat_session_created` event can flush (the POST resolves first; the flush follows ≥250ms later), so the re-render always finds the completed exchange in the store. Reload clears transcripts by design (fresh JS world; the server holds no transcript).
   218	
   219	### 1.8 `reviewCard(opts) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 16
   220	
   221	```js
   222	function reviewCard(opts) { ... }
   223	// opts = {
   224	//   entry:  {entity_id, entity_type, kind, title, waiting_since},   // /api/queues approvals entry
   225	//   detail: <ticket detail JSON | item detail JSON>,                // per-entry fetch by the screen

   227	//   onAccept:        function(payload) -> Promise,  // gating kinds only
   228	//   onApprove:       function() -> Promise,         // kind "review" only
   229	//   onAcceptStatus:  function() -> Promise          // kind "status" only
   230	// }
   231	```
   232	
   233	The card's shell is `panel(entry.title, [...])` — **panel stays the only box primitive** (D11 #2); reviewCard sets its data attributes on the returned section: `[data-review-card] [data-entity-id=<entry.entity_id>] [data-kind=<entry.kind>]` plus `[data-field=<kind>]` when the kind is a gating field name.
   234	
   235	Panel body, per kind (kinds come from the queue: gating field names / `"review"` / `"status"` — carryover.py:84-128):
   236	
   237	- **`success` | `approach` | `plan` | `result`** (gating-field proposal; `entity_type === "ticket"`): head row (state chip via `chip("state", detail.state)`, `chip("pending-proposal")`), then `proposalCard({proposal: detail.fields[kind].proposal, requireGrant: true, newState: advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling), onAccept: opts.onAccept})`. **Guard:** if `detail.fields[kind].proposal` is null (resolved between queue fetch and detail fetch), the screen's vanish path handles it (§3) — reviewCard is never built; the screen checks before constructing.
   238	- **`review`** (needs_review result): head row (`chip("state", detail.state)`), `markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.value)`, then — when `detail.fields.result.notes` is non-null — a `div.review-note` with `markdownBlock(notes)` (the §4.2 review notes: what needs careful checking is the substance of this approval). Action: `button.button--primary [data-approve] "Approve"`. No quick-edit textarea, no grant picker (§4.4.7: approve carries no grant; there is no proposal to edit).
   239	- **`status`** (item status proposal; `entity_type === "item"`): head row showing `chip(null, detail.status)` + `chip(null, "→ " + detail.status_proposal.to_status)`; when `detail.status_proposal.note` is non-null, a `div.review-note` (plain text). Action: `button.button--primary [data-accept-status] "Accept"`. No grant, no textarea (item shape: sprints/views.py:30-51).
   240	
   241	Common footer `div.review-card-actions`: (errorLine slot) + `button.button [data-skip] "Skip"` + — when `entry.entity_type === "ticket"` — `a [data-open-ticket] [href="#/ticket/"+entry.entity_id] "open ticket"` (items have no screen of their own; no link). Approve/Accept-status buttons: disable in flight, rejection → errorLine in the actions row, success → do nothing (the action's events flush → the queue refetch drops the entry → next card renders — "resolved items leave immediately").

    61	   `<script>` tags is a serial-integration step (explicit request in the T17 report), and the
    62	   integrated shell is asserted by the T18 e2e suite (item 32). Amendment A3 closes the gap codex

codex
T15’s plan gives a more specific component contract than T16 assumes: `grantPairPicker(newState, onChange)` exposes `data-grant-atcap`, and `chatPanel(entityId, opts)` is host-fed with gateway status. T16’s plan has a different chat call shape and a separately implemented grant picker, so those are concrete integration mismatches against the adjacent owned plan, not just naming taste.

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan.md:6:**Approach.** Two new classic scripts (`assets/screens-day.js`, `assets/screens-review.js`) register the real `day` and `review` screens over app.js's placeholders (overwrite-wins is the designed mechanism — app.js:26-32 registers placeholders for exactly these names and its comment says T15–T17 scripts, appended after it, re-register before DOMContentLoaded). Five new components land as **additive** entries in `assets/components.js`, in the file's existing style (`make`/`appendChildren`, `createElement`/`textContent` only — the codebase's single `innerHTML` stays in markdown.js). Screens are stateless renders: every action calls `Planner.api.fetchJson` and relies on the WS flush → `route()` → full re-render with fresh fetches (app.js:76-79); no optimistic UI anywhere. The only client state is transient UI state in module scope — chat transcripts + input drafts (components.js scope), review skip memory (screens-review.js scope) — never a cache of canonical entity JSON. `server.py` changes in exactly one place: two script tags in `_SHELL` after app.js. `app.css` gains new class rules, token-only. The gate is `smoke.py` in this ticket folder, modeled on T14's: in-process uvicorn, temp DB, demo seed, headless chromium, numbered `ok NN` checks, `SMOKE PASS`.
orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan.md:15:4. **No client-side state store.** Canonical data is refetched on every render. Transient UI state in module scope is the sanctioned pattern and is limited to exactly: `chatTranscripts`, `chatDrafts` (components.js), `skipped`, `vanished` (screens-review.js). None of these holds server-owned data (chat messages have no server-side representation at all — the server stores only `chat_session_key`, src/planner/chat/service.py:81-104).

orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/plan.md:217:- **Transcript survival mechanism, spelled out:** WS flush → `route()` → the screen re-renders → the screen calls `chatPanel(entityId, ...)` again → the new panel rebuilds its list from module-scope `chatTranscripts[entityId]`, which the flush did not touch. The first send's reply lands *before* its own `chat_session_created` event can flush (the POST resolves first; the flush follows ≥250ms later), so the re-render always finds the completed exchange in the store. Reload clears transcripts by design (fresh JS world; the server holds no transcript).

codex
I found no concrete endpoint path/method/body-shape mismatch. Findings are component split, smoke feasibility, frontend-rule, and plan-internal issues.

1. **blocker** — T16 smoke assumes screen scripts are in the real shell, but they are not.
Evidence: [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:23) says the smoke assumes board/ticket script tags are present; [server.py](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/core/server.py:57) only loads through `/assets/app.js`; [app.js](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/assets/app.js:26) leaves placeholders until screen scripts re-register.
Minimal fix: either make the T16 smoke inject `screens-board.js` and `screens-ticket.js` before navigation, or make the integration step that adds shell tags an explicit prerequisite before smoke.

2. **blocker** — `grantControl` redefines the T15-owned grant-pair picker.
Evidence: D11 says grant-pair picker is component #8 and is embedded in proposal card and grant control [decisions.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/decisions.md:60); T15 owns item #8 [ticket.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T15-day-review/ticket.md:12); T16 builds its own ceiling select and at-cap radios [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:69).
Minimal fix: have T16’s `grantControl` compose the T15 picker, or record an explicit D11 contract change before implementing duplicate picker internals.

3. **should-fix** — Ticket proposal cards require grant on non-gating proposals.
Evidence: T16 says all non-null proposals render and non-gating accepts are allowed [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:198), but then passes `requireGrant: true` on every proposal [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:331). The spec requires the onward grant for gating-field accepts [SPEC.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/SPEC.md:100), and the engine ignores grant only on the non-gating branch [resolution.py](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/tickets/logic/resolution.py:162).
Minimal fix: pass `requireGrant` only when `field === gatingField(detail.state)`; non-gating proposal accepts should not show or require the grant picker.

4. **should-fix** — Board smoke waits on a selector that already matches the nav link.
Evidence: board root uses `[data-screen="board"]` [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:129), and the smoke waits for that selector [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:285), but app shell nav links also set `data-screen` [components.js](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/assets/components.js:52).
Minimal fix: wait for `section.board-screen[data-screen="board"]` or for the six `[data-column]` elements.

5. **blocker** — Grant smoke tries to set a container as if it were a form control.
Evidence: `data-grant-at-cap` is placed on a `div` containing radios [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:77), but the smoke says to set `[data-grant-at-cap] = stop` [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:292).
Minimal fix: either put the data attribute on each radio input, or update the smoke to click `[data-grant-at-cap] input[value="stop"]`.

6. **should-fix** — Link-row text contradicts itself and renders target-side links wrong.
Evidence: the plan says to show both endpoints, but defines text as `from_id -> far-end` [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:221). For a ticket that is the target of a link, `far-end` is `from_id`, so the row becomes `from_id -> from_id`. Demo has exactly that case: flaky blocks review [demo.py](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/src/planner/seed/demo.py:105).
Minimal fix: always render `from_id -> to_id`; separately anchor whichever endpoint is the far ticket.

7. **should-fix** — The smoke’s board-script setup contradicts T16’s file-ownership gate.
Evidence: T16 owned files exclude `server.py` [ticket.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/ticket.md:10), but the smoke requires shell wiring outside those files [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/plan.md:23). T17 handles the same out-of-scope shell issue by injecting screen scripts in smoke [plan.md](/Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2/orchestration/tickets/T17-sprint-backlog/plan.md:449).
Minimal fix: use the same injection pattern or make the shell-wiring integration step explicit and outside T16’s smoke gate.

Verdict: NEEDS-CHANGES.
